// Since all 4 portals share one live order list in this session, a newly placed order
// needs a rider immediately so it actually shows up in that rider's queue when you switch roles.
function autoAssignRider(zoneId) {
  const inZone = RIDERS.filter((r) => r.zone === zoneId && r.status !== "suspended");
  const available = inZone.find((r) => r.status === "available");
  return (available || inZone[0] || RIDERS[0]).id;
}

const fmt = (n) => `Rs. ${Number(n).toLocaleString()}`;
const timeAgo = (ts) => {
  const m = Math.floor((Date.now() - ts) / 60000);
  if (m < 1) return "just now";
  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(m / 60)}h ago`;
};

/* ---------------- Small UI atoms ---------------- */
function Badge({ children, tone = "default" }) {
  const tones = {
    default: "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300",
    orange: "bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700 border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30",
    green: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
    red: "bg-red-50 text-red-600",
    amber: "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700",
  };
  return <span className={`text-xs font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${tones[tone]}`}>{children}</span>;
}

function IconBtn({ icon: Icon, onClick, active }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick}
      className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition ${active ? "bg-orange-500 text-white" : "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 hover:border-orange-300"}`}>
      <Icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
    </button>
  );
}

function PrimaryButton({ children, onClick, full, disabled, small, className = "" }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled}
      className={`${full ? "w-full" : ""} ${small ? "px-4 py-2.5 text-sm" : "px-6 py-3.5 text-[15px]"} bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600 active:bg-orange-700 disabled:bg-stone-200 disabled:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold shadow-sm shadow-orange-500/20 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function GhostButton({ children, onClick, full, small, className = "" }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick}
      className={`${full ? "w-full" : ""} ${small ? "px-4 py-2.5 text-sm" : "px-6 py-3.5 text-[15px]"} bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 active:bg-orange-100 text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl font-semibold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/* Receipt-style ticket card — the signature element: dashed perforation + mono amounts */
function TicketCard({ children, className = "" }) {
  return (
    <div className={`relative bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden ${className}`}>
      <div className="absolute -left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-[#FAF9F6] border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700" />
      <div className="absolute -right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-[#FAF9F6] border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700" />
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- App Shell ---------------- */
export default function App() {
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState("landing"); // landing | roleselect
  const [role, setRole] = useState(null); // null | customer | shopkeeper | rider | admin
  const [lang, setLang] = useState("en");
  const [dark, setDark] = useState(false);
  const [confirmExit, setConfirmExit] = useState(false);
  const [allOrders, setAllOrders] = useState(SEED_ORDERS); // shared across all 4 portals — no backend needed since it's one app instance
  const [withdrawals, setWithdrawals] = useState([
    { id: "w1", riderId: "r1", rider: "Asad Ali", amount: 2000, status: "pending" },
    { id: "w2", riderId: "r3", rider: "Usman Tariq", amount: 3500, status: "pending" },
  ]);
  const [adminAccount, setAdminAccount] = useState(DEFAULT_ADMIN_ACCOUNT); // shared — Admin edits, everyone else sees it for top-ups
  const [serviceProviders, setServiceProviders] = useState([]); // shared — new provider signups go here, Admin approves
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState(RIDERS); // shared — rider's own profile edits reflect for Admin & Customer instantly
  const [customerProfile, setCustomerProfile] = useState({ name: "Sana Ahmed", phone: "0300 1234567" }); // shared — customer's own edits reflect for Rider & Admin instantly
  const t = T[lang];

  const requestExit = () => setConfirmExit(true);

  return (
    <div className={dark ? "dark" : ""}>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#FAF9F6] dark:bg-stone-900 text-stone-900 dark:text-stone-100 font-[Inter] transition-colors">
        <style>{`
          @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Sora:wght@600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@500&display=swap');
          .font-display { font-family: 'Sora', sans-serif; }
          .font-mono-tk { font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace; }
          * { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; }

          /* Dark mode — direct overrides (not relying on Tailwind's dark: variant) */
          .dark { background-color: #1c1917; color: #f5f5f4; }
          .dark .bg-\\[\\#FAF9F6\\] { background-color: #1c1917 !important; }
          .dark .bg-white { background-color: #292524 !important; }
          .dark .bg-stone-50 { background-color: #3f3a37 !important; }
          .dark .bg-stone-100 { background-color: #44403c !important; }
          .dark .border-stone-200 { border-color: #44403c !important; }
          .dark .border-stone-100 { border-color: #3f3a37 !important; }
          .dark .border-orange-100 { border-color: rgba(249,115,22,0.25) !important; }
          .dark .border-orange-200 { border-color: rgba(249,115,22,0.35) !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-900 { color: #fafaf9 !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-800 { color: #f5f5f4 !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-700 { color: #e7e5e4 !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-600 { color: #d6d3d1 !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-500 { color: #a8a29e !important; }
          .dark .text-stone-400 { color: #8a8480 !important; }
          .dark .bg-orange-50 { background-color: rgba(249,115,22,0.12) !important; }
          .dark .bg-black\\/40 { background-color: rgba(0,0,0,0.6) !important; }
          .dark ::placeholder { color: #78716c !important; }

          /* Fix: inputs/textareas must always inherit transparent bg + explicit readable text color */
          textarea, input, select { background-color: transparent; color: #1c1917; }
          .dark textarea, .dark input, .dark select { color: #f5f5f4; }

          /* Auto-growing textarea for the order composer */
          .autogrow { overflow: hidden; min-height: 90px; }
        `}</style>
        {screen === "landing" && <LandingPage onStart={() => setScreen("roleselect")} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} t={t} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} />}
        {screen === "roleselect" && !role && <RoleSelect onSelect={setRole} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} t={t} onBackHome={() => setScreen("landing")} />}
        {role === "customer" && <CustomerApp t={t} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={requestExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} allOrders={allOrders} setAllOrders={setAllOrders} adminAccount={adminAccount} riders={riders} customerProfile={customerProfile} setCustomerProfile={setCustomerProfile} />}
        {role === "shopkeeper" && <ShopkeeperApp t={t} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={requestExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} allOrders={allOrders} setAllOrders={setAllOrders} adminAccount={adminAccount} />}
        {role === "provider" && <ServiceProviderApp t={t} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={requestExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} adminAccount={adminAccount} providers={serviceProviders} setProviders={setServiceProviders} />}
        {role === "rider" && <RiderApp t={t} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={requestExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} allOrders={allOrders} setAllOrders={setAllOrders} withdrawals={withdrawals} setWithdrawals={setWithdrawals} adminAccount={adminAccount} riders={riders} setRiders={setRiders} customerProfile={customerProfile} />}
        {role === "admin" && <AdminApp t={t} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={requestExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark} allOrders={allOrders} setAllOrders={setAllOrders} withdrawals={withdrawals} setWithdrawals={setWithdrawals} adminAccount={adminAccount} setAdminAccount={setAdminAccount} serviceProviders={serviceProviders} setServiceProviders={setServiceProviders} riders={riders} setRiders={setRiders} customerProfile={customerProfile} />}

        {confirmExit && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-xs w-full text-center">
              <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                <LogOut size={18} className="text-red-500" strokeWidth={1.8} />
              </div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100 mb-1">Log out?</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-5">You'll need to sign in again to continue.</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <GhostButton full onClick={() => setConfirmExit(false)}>No, stay</GhostButton>
                <button onClick={() => { setConfirmExit(false); setRole(null); }}
                  className="w-full px-5 py-3 bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl font-medium transition">
                  Yes, log out
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Landing Page ---------------- */
function LandingPage({ onStart, lang, setLang, t, dark, setDark }) {
  const howItWorks = [
    { icon: FileText, title: "Tell us what you need", desc: "Text, photo, voice note, or video — describe your order however's easiest for you" },
    { icon: Bike, title: "A rider picks it up", desc: "Our nearest available rider buys it fresh from your favourite local shops" },
    { icon: CheckCircle2, title: "It's at your door", desc: "Track it live and pay however you like — cash on delivery or online" },
  ];

  const customerBenefits = [
    { icon: Zap, text: "Order in seconds — just describe it, no menus to browse" },
    { icon: Timer, text: "Live delivery timer for every single order, area by area" },
    { icon: Wallet, text: "Wallet-based billing — never overpay, ever" },
    { icon: Star, text: "Rate every delivery, only work with riders you trust" },
  ];
  const shopkeeperBenefits = [
    { icon: Wallet, text: "Lower delivery charges than a regular customer order" },
    { icon: Timer, text: "Faster pickup — jump the queue with priority timing" },
    { icon: ListOrdered, text: "Place multiple orders a day, all tracked in one dashboard" },
    { icon: Users, text: "Grow your customer base with automatic outreach" },
  ];

  const testimonials = [
    { name: "Sana A.", role: "Customer, Gulberg", text: "I just voice-note my grocery list and it shows up in 25 minutes. Genuinely easier than going myself." },
    { name: "Rahim Store", role: "Shopkeeper, Model Town", text: "My delivery costs dropped and my customers get their orders faster. Taskly basically became our delivery team." },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* ambient background glow for depth */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-40 -right-40 w-[32rem] h-[32rem] bg-orange-200/40 dark:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-96 -left-40 w-[28rem] h-[28rem] bg-orange-100/50 dark:bg-orange-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl" />

      {/* Nav */}
      <div className="relative max-w-6xl mx-auto px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">

function LandingOrderDemo() {
  const steps = [
    { key: "placed", label: "Order Placed", icon: Clock, desc: "You described your order — text, photo or voice" },
    { key: "preparing", label: "Preparing", icon: Package, desc: "Your rider is at the shop, buying everything" },
    { key: "out", label: "Out for Delivery", icon: Bike, desc: "Bill uploaded — rider is on the way to you" },
    { key: "delivered", label: "Delivered", icon: CheckCircle2, desc: "Rate your rider and you're all done" },
  ];
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);

  return (
    <div className="relative max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 py-16">
      <p className="text-center text-xs font-semibold text-orange-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">See it in action</p>
      <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-2xl md:text-3xl text-center mb-2">Tap through a live order</h2>
      <p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center mb-10">This is exactly what you'll see, step by step, once you place an order</p>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
          {steps.map((s, i) => (
            <React.Fragment key={s.key}>
              <button onClick={() => setActive(i)} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 group">
                <div className={`w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300 ${i <= active ? "bg-orange-500 text-white shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/30 scale-105" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-500 group-hover:bg-stone-200 dark:group-hover:bg-stone-600"}`}>
                  <s.icon size={18} strokeWidth={2} />
                </div>
                <span className={`text-[10px] md:text-xs font-medium hidden sm:block ${i <= active ? "text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200" : "text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-600"}`}>{s.label}</span>
              </button>
              {i < steps.length - 1 && <div className={`flex-1 h-0.5 mx-1 md:mx-2 transition-colors duration-300 ${i < active ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700"}`} />}
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-900 rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-orange-500 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/20">
            {React.createElement(steps[active].icon, { size: 24, className: "text-white" })}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100 mb-0.5">{steps[active].label}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{steps[active].desc}</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setActive((a) => Math.min(a + 1, steps.length - 1))}
            disabled={active === steps.length - 1}
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 disabled:opacity-30 hover:border-orange-300 transition">
            <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-stone-500" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function LandingFAQ() {
  const faqs = [
    { q: "How is Taskly different from Foodpanda or other apps?", a: "Those apps only work with a fixed catalog. With Taskly, you can describe literally anything — a handwritten prescription, a specific grocery brand, a document to print — and a rider goes and gets it for you." },
    { q: "How do I know the bill is accurate?", a: "Your rider photographs the actual shop invoice before you pay. You see the exact same picture and amount they paid, plus the delivery charge and platform fee — all itemized." },
    { q: "What if I get overcharged or undercharged?", a: "Any difference is automatically adjusted in your Taskly wallet and applied to your next order — or you can dispute the bill directly from the order screen." },
    { q: "Can I pay online instead of cash?", a: "Yes — you'll see your rider's account details, transfer the amount, and upload a screenshot. Once confirmed, your order continues." },
    { q: "How do I register my shop on Taskly?", a: "Tap 'Partner as a Shopkeeper', set up your shop profile (name, address, contact, photos), and you can start placing pickup-and-drop orders immediately — plus optionally list your full menu for customers to order from directly." },
  ];
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(0);

  return (
    <div className="relative max-w-3xl mx-auto px-6 py-16">
      <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-2xl md:text-3xl text-center mb-10">Frequently asked questions</h2>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {faqs.map((f, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <button onClick={() => setOpen(open === i ? -1 : i)} className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 text-left">
              <span className="text-sm font-medium pr-4">{f.q}</span>
              <ChevronRight size={16} className={`text-stone-400 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${open === i ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
            </button>
            {open === i && <p className="px-5 pb-4 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 leading-relaxed">{f.a}</p>}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Role Select / Login ---------------- */
function RoleSelect({ onSelect, lang, setLang, t, onBackHome }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState("pick"); // pick | phone | otp
  const [pendingRole, setPendingRole] = useState(null);
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");

  const roles = [
    { key: "customer", icon: Home, label: t.customer, desc: t.customerDesc, needsAuth: true },
    { key: "shopkeeper", icon: Store, label: t.shopkeeper, desc: t.shopkeeperDesc, needsAuth: true },
    { key: "provider", icon: Settings, label: "Service Provider", desc: "Offer plumbing, electrical & other home services", needsAuth: true },
    { key: "rider", icon: Bike, label: t.rider, desc: t.riderDesc, needsAuth: false },
    { key: "admin", icon: Settings, label: t.admin, desc: t.adminDesc, needsAuth: false },
  ];

  const pick = (r) => {
    if (r.needsAuth) { setPendingRole(r.key); setStep("phone"); }
    else onSelect(r.key);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 relative">
      {onBackHome && (
        <button onClick={onBackHome} className="absolute top-6 left-6 flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-600 transition">
          <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Home
        </button>
      )}
      <button onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")}
        className="absolute top-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:text-orange-600 transition">
        <Globe size={15} strokeWidth={1.8} /> {lang === "en" ? "اردو" : "English"}
      </button>

      <div className="w-full max-w-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 justify-center">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-orange-500 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Package size={20} className="text-white" strokeWidth={2} />
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-3xl font-bold text-stone-900 dark:text-stone-50">{t.appName}</h1>
        </div>
        <p className="text-center text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-sm mb-10">{t.tagline}</p>

        {step === "pick" && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-medium mb-3 text-center">{t.chooseRole}</p>
            {roles.map((r) => (
              <button key={r.key} onClick={() => pick(r)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 hover:border-orange-300 hover:shadow-md rounded-2xl p-4 transition text-left group">
                <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center group-hover:bg-orange-500 transition">
                  <r.icon size={20} className="text-orange-500 group-hover:text-white transition" strokeWidth={1.8} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100">{r.label}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{r.desc}</div>
                </div>
                <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-stone-300" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {step === "phone" && (
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-6">
            <button onClick={() => setStep("pick")} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-sm">
              <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back
            </button>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-2 block">{t.phone}</label>
            <input value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="03XX XXXXXXX"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 mb-4 outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk text-sm" />
            <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => setStep("otp")} disabled={phone.length < 4}>{t.sendOtp}</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === "otp" && (
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-6">
            <button onClick={() => setStep("phone")} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-sm">
              <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back
            </button>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-2 block">{t.otp}</label>
            <input maxLength={4} placeholder="••••" defaultValue="1234"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 mb-4 outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk text-lg tracking-[0.5em] text-center" />
            <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => onSelect(pendingRole)}>{t.verify}</PrimaryButton>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center mt-3">Demo OTP is pre-filled — just tap continue</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Top Bar (shared, mobile) ---------------- */
function MobileTopBar({ title, lang, setLang, onExit, subtitle, notifications, onNotificationClick, dark, setDark }) {
  const [showInbox, setShowInbox] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-[#FAF9F6]/90 backdrop-blur border-b border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
      <div>
        <h1 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-stone-900 dark:text-stone-50">{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {notifications && (
          <div className="relative">
            <IconBtn icon={Bell} onClick={() => setShowInbox((s) => !s)} />
            {notifications.length > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 rounded-full text-[9px] text-white flex items-center justify-center">{notifications.length}</span>}
            {showInbox && (
              <div className="fixed top-16 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-4 sm:w-80 max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-2 z-50">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 px-3 py-2">Notifications</p>
                {notifications.map((n, i) => (
                  <button key={i} onClick={() => { setShowInbox(false); onNotificationClick && onNotificationClick(n); }}
                    className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-xl">
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{n.text}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{n.time}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        <IconBtn icon={Globe} onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")} />
        {setDark && <IconBtn icon={dark ? Sun : Moon} onClick={() => setDark((d) => !d)} />}
        <IconBtn icon={LogOut} onClick={onExit} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function BottomNav({ tabs, active, setActive }) {
  const tight = tabs.length > 5;
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-20 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border-t border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-1 py-2 flex justify-around max-w-md mx-auto">
      {tabs.map((tab) => (
        <button key={tab.key} onClick={() => setActive(tab.key)}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 ${tight ? "px-1.5" : "px-4"} py-1.5 rounded-xl transition min-w-0 ${active === tab.key ? "text-orange-600" : "text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500"}`}>
          <tab.icon size={tight ? 18 : 20} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          <span className={`${tight ? "text-[9px]" : "text-[10px]"} font-medium whitespace-nowrap`}>{tab.label}</span>
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   CUSTOMER APP
   ============================================================ */
function CustomerApp({ t, lang, setLang, onExit, dark, setDark, allOrders, setAllOrders, adminAccount, riders, customerProfile, setCustomerProfile }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("home");
  const orders = allOrders.filter((o) => o.type === "customer" || o.type === "storeOrder");
  const setOrders = (updater) => {
    setAllOrders((prevAll) => {
      const mine = prevAll.filter((o) => o.type === "customer" || o.type === "storeOrder");
      const others = prevAll.filter((o) => o.type !== "customer" && o.type !== "storeOrder");
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(mine) : updater;
      return [...next, ...others];
    });
  };
  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState(-150);
  const [openOrder, setOpenOrder] = useState(null);
  const [favShops, setFavShops] = useState(["Meat Corner", "Al-Shifa Pharmacy"]);
  const [showInbox, setShowInbox] = useState(false);
  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState([
    { id: 1, type: "Home", label: "", address: "House 12, Street 4, Gulshan", zone: "z1" },
    { id: 2, type: "Office", label: "", address: "Plot 88, DHA Phase 5", zone: "z2" },
  ]);
  // Draft persists across tab switches so nothing typed is ever lost
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({
    step: "compose", text: "", attachments: [], zone: ZONES[0].id, urgent: false, notes: "", addressIdx: 0,
  });
  const notifications = [
    { text: "Your order TK-1042 is out for delivery", time: "18m ago", orderId: "TK-1042" },
    { text: "Rider Asad Ali started preparing your order", time: "22m ago", orderId: "TK-1042" },
    { text: "You earned Rs. 50 loyalty points on TK-1039", time: "26h ago", orderId: "TK-1039" },
    { text: "New promo: FIRST20 — 20% off your next order", time: "2d ago", tab: "profile" },
  ];

  const openNotification = (n) => {
    setShowInbox(false);
    if (n.orderId) { setTab("orders"); setOpenOrder(n.orderId); }
    else if (n.tab) { setTab(n.tab); }
  };

  const tabs = [
    { key: "home", label: t.newOrder, icon: Plus },
    { key: "stores", label: "Stores", icon: Store },
    { key: "services", label: "Services", icon: Timer },
    { key: "orders", label: t.orders, icon: ListOrdered },
    { key: "wallet", label: t.wallet, icon: Wallet },
    { key: "profile", label: t.profile, icon: User },
  ];

  const addOrder = (order) => {
    setOrders((prev) => [{ ...order, riderId: order.riderId || autoAssignRider(order.zone) }, ...prev]);
    setDraft({ step: "compose", text: "", attachments: [], zone: ZONES[0].id, urgent: false, notes: "", addressIdx: 0 });
    setTab("orders");
  };

  const checkoutCart = (byStore) => {
    const defaultZone = addresses[0]?.zone || ZONES[0].id;
    const zoneData = ZONES.find(z => z.id === defaultZone);
    const created = Object.entries(byStore).map(([shopName, items]) => {
      const total = items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.price * i.qty, 0);
      return {
        id: "TK-" + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000), type: "storeOrder", zone: defaultZone,
        status: "placed", riderId: autoAssignRider(defaultZone), shopName,
        pickup: { shopName, shopAddress: `${shopName}, ${zoneData?.name || ""}` },
        dropAddress: addresses[0]?.label, dropPhone: "0300 1234567",
        items, text: items.map((i) => `${i.qty}x ${i.name}`).join(", "),
        images: [], urgent: false, createdAt: Date.now(),
        bill: { amount: total, uploaded: true, deliveryCharge: zoneData?.charge || 0, platformFee: zoneData?.platformFee || 0, total: total + (zoneData?.charge || 0) + (zoneData?.platformFee || 0) },
        payment: { method: "cash", status: "pending" }, chat: [],
      };
    });
    setOrders((prev) => [...created, ...prev]);
    setTab("orders");
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen relative pb-24">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-[#FAF9F6]/90 backdrop-blur border-b border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-stone-900 dark:text-stone-50">{tab === "home" ? t.appName : t[tab] || "Taskly"}</h1>
          {tab === "home" && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Gulberg Zone · Service Available</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <IconBtn icon={Bell} onClick={() => setShowInbox((s) => !s)} />
            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 rounded-full text-[9px] text-white flex items-center justify-center">4</span>
            {showInbox && (
              <div className="fixed top-16 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-4 sm:w-80 max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-2 z-50">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 px-3 py-2">Notifications</p>
                {notifications.map((n, i) => (
                  <button key={i} onClick={() => openNotification(n)} className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-xl">
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{n.text}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{n.time}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <IconBtn icon={Globe} onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")} />
          <IconBtn icon={dark ? Sun : Moon} onClick={() => setDark((d) => !d)} />
          <IconBtn icon={LogOut} onClick={onExit} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {tab === "home" && <CustomerHome t={t} onOrderCreated={addOrder} favShops={favShops} draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} addresses={addresses} setAddresses={setAddresses} />}
      {tab === "stores" && <CustomerStores onCheckout={checkoutCart} />}
      {tab === "services" && <HomeServicesView inline />}
      {tab === "orders" && (
        <CustomerOrders t={t} orders={orders} setOrders={setOrders} openOrder={openOrder} setOpenOrder={setOpenOrder}
          wallet={wallet} setWallet={setWallet} riders={riders} />
      )}
      {tab === "wallet" && <CustomerWallet t={t} wallet={wallet} setWallet={setWallet} orders={orders} adminAccount={adminAccount} />}
      {tab === "profile" && <CustomerProfile t={t} favShops={favShops} profile={customerProfile} setProfile={setCustomerProfile} addresses={addresses} setAddresses={setAddresses} />}

      {tab !== "orders" && (() => {
        const active = orders.find((o) => o.status !== "delivered" && !["cancelled", "rejected"].includes(o.status));
        if (!active) return null;
        const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === active.zone);
        return (
          <button onClick={() => { setTab("orders"); setOpenOrder(active.id); }}
            className="fixed bottom-20 left-4 right-4 max-w-md mx-auto z-30 bg-stone-900 dark:bg-orange-500 text-white rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 shadow-xl flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white/15 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Bike size={16} /></div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-left">
              <p className="text-xs font-medium truncate">{active.id} · {STATUS_LABEL[active.status]}</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-white/70">{zone?.name}, {CITY}</p>
            </div>
            <ChevronRight size={16} className="shrink-0" />
          </button>
        );
      })()}

      <BottomNav tabs={tabs} active={tab} setActive={setTab} />
    </div>
  );
}
