function CustomerStores({ onCheckout }) {
  const [catFilter, setCatFilter] = useState("All");
  const [openStore, setOpenStore] = useState(null);
  const [storeSearch, setStoreSearch] = useState("");
  const [subCat, setSubCat] = useState("All");
  const [cart, setCart] = useState({}); // { productId: {name, price, qty, shopName} }
  const [showCart, setShowCart] = useState(false);

  const addToCart = (product, shopName) => {
    setCart((prev) => {
      const existing = prev[product.id];
      return { ...prev, [product.id]: { name: product.name, price: product.price, shopName, qty: (existing?.qty || 0) + 1 } };
    });
  };
  const removeFromCart = (id) => setCart((prev) => {
    const next = { ...prev };
    if (next[id].qty <= 1) delete next[id]; else next[id] = { ...next[id], qty: next[id].qty - 1 };
    return next;
  });

  const cartItems = Object.entries(cart).map(([id, v]) => ({ id, ...v }));
  const cartCount = cartItems.reduce((s, i) => s + i.qty, 0);
  const cartTotal = cartItems.reduce((s, i) => s + i.price * i.qty, 0);
  const storeCount = new Set(cartItems.map((i) => i.shopName)).size;

  const doCheckout = () => {
    const byStore = {};
    cartItems.forEach((i) => { (byStore[i.shopName] = byStore[i.shopName] || []).push(i); });
    onCheckout(byStore);
  };

  const CartFab = () => cartCount > 0 && !showCart && (
    <button onClick={() => setShowCart(true)} className="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-orange-500 text-white rounded-full px-5 py-3 shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium z-30">
      <ShoppingCartIconInline /> {cartCount} item{cartCount > 1 ? "s" : ""} · {fmt(cartTotal)}
    </button>
  );

  if (openStore) {
    const store = DEMO_STORES.find((s) => s.id === openStore);
    const subCats = ["All", ...new Set(store.products.map((p) => p.category))];
    const visibleProducts = store.products
      .filter((p) => subCat === "All" || p.category === subCat)
      .filter((p) => !storeSearch.trim() || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(storeSearch.trim().toLowerCase()));

    return (
      <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-4 pb-24">
        <button onClick={() => { setOpenStore(null); setSubCat("All"); setStoreSearch(""); }} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-600">
          <ChevronLeft size={16} /> All stores
        </button>
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl">{store.name}</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{store.category}</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5">
          <Search size={15} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" />
          <input value={storeSearch} onChange={(e) => setStoreSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder={`Search in ${store.name}...`}
            className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm outline-none bg-transparent" />
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
          {subCats.map((c) => (
            <button key={c} onClick={() => setSubCat(c)}
              className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border transition ${subCat === c ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
              {c}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {visibleProducts.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No items match your search</p>}
          {visibleProducts.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                {p.photo ? <img src={p.photo} alt="" className="w-full h-full rounded-lg object-cover" /> : <ImageIcon size={16} className="text-stone-300" />}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{p.name}</p>
                {p.detail && <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 truncate">{p.detail}</p>}
                <p className="text-xs font-mono-tk text-orange-600 mt-0.5">{fmt(p.price)}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {cart[p.id]?.qty > 0 && (
                  <>
                    <button onClick={() => removeFromCart(p.id)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center">-</button>
                    <span className="text-sm font-mono-tk w-4 text-center">{cart[p.id].qty}</span>
                  </>
                )}
                <button onClick={() => addToCart(p, store.name)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-orange-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center"><Plus size={13} /></button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <CartFab />
        {showCart && <CustomerCartSheet cartItems={cartItems} cartTotal={cartTotal} storeCount={storeCount} onClose={() => setShowCart(false)} onCheckout={doCheckout} addToCart={addToCart} removeFromCart={removeFromCart} DEMO_STORES={DEMO_STORES} />}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const filteredStores = DEMO_STORES.filter((s) => catFilter === "All" || s.category === catFilter);

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-4 pb-24">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">Local Stores</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Order directly from your favourite shops — mix items from multiple stores in one checkout</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setShowCart(true)} className="relative w-10 h-10 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center">
          <ShoppingCartIconInline />
          {cartCount > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 rounded-full text-[9px] text-white flex items-center justify-center">{cartCount}</span>}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {STORE_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
          <button key={c} onClick={() => setCatFilter(c)}
            className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border transition ${catFilter === c ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
            {c}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {filteredStores.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No stores in this category yet</p>}
        {filteredStores.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.id} onClick={() => setOpenStore(s.id)} className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Store size={19} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.category} · {s.products.length} items</p>
            </div>
            <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-stone-300" />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <CartFab />
      {showCart && <CustomerCartSheet cartItems={cartItems} cartTotal={cartTotal} storeCount={storeCount} onClose={() => setShowCart(false)} onCheckout={doCheckout} addToCart={addToCart} removeFromCart={removeFromCart} DEMO_STORES={DEMO_STORES} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function ShoppingCartIconInline() {
  return (
    <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
      <circle cx="8" cy="21" r="1" /><circle cx="19" cy="21" r="1" />
      <path d="M2.05 2.05h2l2.66 12.42a2 2 0 0 0 2 1.58h9.78a2 2 0 0 0 2-1.58l1.65-7.42H5.12" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function CustomerCartSheet({ cartItems, cartTotal, storeCount, onClose, onCheckout, addToCart, removeFromCart, DEMO_STORES }) {
  const findProduct = (id) => {
    for (const s of DEMO_STORES) { const p = s.products.find((pr) => pr.id === id); if (p) return p; }
    return null;
  };
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-end justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4 max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="font-display font-bold">Your Cart</p>
          <button onClick={onClose}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
        </div>
        {cartItems.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">Your cart is empty</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            {storeCount > 1 && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Items from {storeCount} stores — checkout creates a separate order for each</p>}
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {cartItems.map((i) => (
                <div key={i.id} className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                  <div>
                    <p>{i.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{i.shopName} · {fmt(i.price)} each</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => removeFromCart(i.id)} className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center text-xs">-</button>
                    <span className="font-mono-tk w-4 text-center">{i.qty}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => { const p = findProduct(i.id); if (p) addToCart(p, i.shopName); }} className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-orange-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs">+</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 pt-3 flex justify-between font-semibold">
              <span>Total</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(cartTotal)}</span>
            </div>
            <PrimaryButton full onClick={onCheckout}>Checkout ({storeCount} store{storeCount > 1 ? "s" : ""})</PrimaryButton>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerHome({ t, onOrderCreated, favShops, draft, setDraft, addresses, setAddresses }) {
  if (draft.step === "address") return <CustomerAddressStep draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} addresses={addresses} setAddresses={setAddresses} />;
  if (draft.step === "review") return <CustomerReviewStep draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} addresses={addresses} onOrderCreated={onOrderCreated} />;
  return <CustomerComposeStep t={t} draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} addresses={addresses} />;
}

function CustomerComposeStep({ t, draft, setDraft, addresses }) {
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recSeconds, setRecSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [micError, setMicError] = useState(false);
  const taRef = useRef(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef(null);
  const chunksRef = useRef([]);
  const timerRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (taRef.current) {
      taRef.current.style.height = "auto";
      taRef.current.style.height = taRef.current.scrollHeight + "px";
    }
  }, [draft.text]);

  const setText = (text) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, text }));
  const setNotes = (notes) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, notes }));
  const setUrgent = (urgent) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, urgent }));

  const addAttachment = (att) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, attachments: [...d.attachments, { caption: "", ...att }] }));
  const updateCaption = (i, caption) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, attachments: d.attachments.map((a, idx) => idx === i ? { ...a, caption } : a) }));
  const removeAttachment = (i) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, attachments: d.attachments.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i) }));

  const handleFile = (file, kind) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const isVideo = file.type.startsWith("video");
    const isImage = file.type.startsWith("image");
    addAttachment({
      type: kind === "doc" ? "doc" : isVideo ? "video" : "photo",
      label: file.name,
      previewUrl: (isImage || isVideo) ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : undefined,
      isVideo,
    });
  };

  const startRecording = async () => {
    setMicError(false);
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      chunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => chunksRef.current.push(e.data);
      recorder.onstop = () => {
        const blob = new Blob(chunksRef.current, { type: "audio/webm" });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        addAttachment({ type: "voice", label: `Voice note — ${recSeconds}s`, audioUrl: url });
        stream.getTracks().forEach((tr) => tr.stop());
      };
      mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start();
      setRecording(true);
      setRecSeconds(0);
      timerRef.current = setInterval(() => setRecSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
    } catch (err) {
      setMicError(true);
      // Fallback so the flow still works if mic access isn't available in this preview
      addAttachment({ type: "voice", label: "Voice note — 0:14 (simulated, mic unavailable)" });
    }
  };

  const stopRecording = () => {
    clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    setRecording(false);
    mediaRecorderRef.current?.stop();
  };

  const canContinue = draft.text.trim().length > 0 || draft.attachments.length > 0;
  const attachIcon = { photo: Camera, doc: FileText, voice: Mic, video: Package };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      {(() => {
        const defaultZone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === addresses?.[0]?.zone);
        if (defaultZone?.status === "paused") {
          return (
            <div className="relative [perspective:600px]">
              <div className="absolute inset-x-1 top-1 h-full bg-red-700 rounded-2xl opacity-40 [transform:rotateX(8deg)_translateZ(-8px)]" />
              <div className="relative bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-red-600 text-white rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 flex items-center gap-3 shadow-lg shadow-red-500/30 overflow-hidden [transform:rotateX(3deg)]">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/15 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 animate-pulse">
                  <AlertCircle size={16} className="text-white" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide opacity-80">{defaultZone.name} — Service Paused</p>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{defaultZone.reason}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        }
        return null;
      })()}
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">{t.whatDoYouNeed}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Mix text, photos, videos, voice notes & documents — all in one order</p>
      </div>

      <TicketCard className="p-4 space-y-4">
        <textarea ref={taRef} value={draft.text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} rows={3}
          placeholder="e.g.&#10;2kg onion&#10;1kg garlic&#10;2kg tomatoes&#10;...write as long a list as you need, it will all fit"
          className="autogrow w-full outline-none resize-none text-sm leading-relaxed placeholder:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" />

        {/* Attach toolbar — label-based, works reliably everywhere, combine freely */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
          <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 py-3.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition cursor-pointer">
            <Camera size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} /> <span className="text-[11px] font-medium">Camera</span>
            <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => { handleFile(e.target.files?.[0], "photo"); e.target.value = ""; }} />
          </label>
          <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 py-3.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition cursor-pointer">
            <ImageIcon size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} /> <span className="text-[11px] font-medium">Gallery</span>
            <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => { handleFile(e.target.files?.[0], "photo"); e.target.value = ""; }} />
          </label>
          <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 py-3.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition cursor-pointer">
            <FileText size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} /> <span className="text-[11px] font-medium">Document</span>
            <input type="file" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt" className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => { handleFile(e.target.files?.[0], "doc"); e.target.value = ""; }} />
          </label>
          <button type="button" onClick={recording ? stopRecording : startRecording}
            className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 py-3.5 rounded-xl border transition ${recording ? "bg-red-500 border-red-500 text-white animate-pulse" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10"}`}>
            <Mic size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} /> <span className="text-[11px] font-medium">{recording ? `${recSeconds}s · Stop` : "Voice"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        {micError && <p className="text-xs text-amber-600">Microphone isn't accessible in this preview — a placeholder voice note was added instead. Recording works normally once deployed on a real device.</p>}

        {/* Attachment previews with captions */}
        {draft.attachments.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
            {draft.attachments.map((att, i) => {
              const Icon = attachIcon[att.type];
              return (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-2.5">
                  {att.previewUrl ? (
                    <div className="relative shrink-0">
                      <img src={att.previewUrl} alt="" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg object-cover border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30" />
                      {att.isVideo && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center"><Package size={14} className="text-white" /></div>}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Icon size={17} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1 truncate">{att.label}</p>
                    {att.audioUrl && <audio src={att.audioUrl} controls className="w-full h-8 mb-1.5" />}
                    <input value={att.caption} onChange={(e) => updateCaption(i, e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Add a caption (optional)..."
                      className="w-full text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 outline-none focus:border-orange-300" />
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => removeAttachment(i)} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:text-red-500 shrink-0"><X size={15} /></button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </TicketCard>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-2 block">{t.specialInstructions}</label>
        <input value={draft.notes} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Don't ring the bell, call instead"
          className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-orange-400 text-sm" />
      </div>

      <button onClick={() => setUrgent(!draft.urgent)}
        className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl border transition ${draft.urgent ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800"}`}>
        <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center ${draft.urgent ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700"}`}>
          <Zap size={17} className={draft.urgent ? "text-white" : "text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500"} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        </div>
        <div className="text-left flex-1">
          <div className="text-sm font-medium">{t.urgent}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{t.urgentDesc}</div>
        </div>
        <div className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${draft.urgent ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-500" : "border-stone-300"}`}>
          {draft.urgent && <Check size={12} className="text-white" />}
        </div>
      </button>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-2 block">When should this be delivered?</label>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, scheduledFor: null }))}
            className={`py-3 rounded-xl border text-sm font-medium transition ${!draft.scheduledFor ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"}`}>
            Now (ASAP)
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, scheduledFor: d.scheduledFor || "later-today" }))}
            className={`py-3 rounded-xl border text-sm font-medium transition ${draft.scheduledFor ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"}`}>
            Schedule for later
          </button>
        </div>
        {draft.scheduledFor && (
          <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
            <input type="date" value={draft.scheduledDate || ""} onChange={(e) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, scheduledDate: e.target.value }))}
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input type="time" value={draft.scheduledTime || ""} onChange={(e) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, scheduledTime: e.target.value }))}
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <PrimaryButton full disabled={!canContinue} onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, step: "address" }))}>
        Review Order <ChevronRight size={17} />
      </PrimaryButton>
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerAddressStep({ draft, setDraft, addresses, setAddresses }) {
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [newType, setNewType] = useState("Home");
  const [newText, setNewText] = useState("");
  const [newZone, setNewZone] = useState(ZONES[0].id);
  const ADDRESS_TYPES = [{ key: "Home", icon: Home }, { key: "Office", icon: Briefcase }, { key: "Other", icon: MapPin }];

  const saveNewAddress = () => {
    if (!newText.trim()) return;
    setAddresses((prev) => [...prev, { id: Date.now(), type: newType, label: "", address: newText, zone: newZone }]);
    setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, addressIdx: addresses.length, zone: newZone }));
    setNewText(""); setAdding(false);
  };

  const selectAddress = (i) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, addressIdx: i, zone: addresses[i].zone }));

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <button onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, step: "compose" }))} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-600">
        <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back to order details
      </button>

      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">Delivery address</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Delivery charges are fixed per {CITY} area, set by Taskly</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2.5">
        {addresses.map((a, i) => {
          const z = ZONES.find((zn) => zn.id === a.zone);
          const TypeIcon = ADDRESS_TYPES.find((tp) => tp.key === a.type)?.icon || MapPin;
          return (
            <div key={a.id ?? i}>
              <button onClick={() => selectAddress(i)}
                className={`w-full text-left flex items-start gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl border transition ${draft.addressIdx === i ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800"}`}>
                <TypeIcon size={18} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100">{a.type}{a.label && <span className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-normal"> · {a.label}</span>}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mt-0.5">{a.address}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{z?.name}, {CITY} · delivery {fmt(z?.charge || 0)}{z?.status === "paused" && <span className="text-red-500 font-medium"> · Currently closed</span>}</p>
                </div>
                <div className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${draft.addressIdx === i ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-500" : "border-stone-300"}`}>
                  {draft.addressIdx === i && <Check size={12} className="text-white" />}
                </div>
              </button>
              {draft.addressIdx === i && z?.status === "paused" && (
                <div className="mt-2 bg-red-50 border border-red-100 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2">
                  <AlertCircle size={15} className="text-red-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600">{z.reason}. Your order can be scheduled and picked up once service resumes.</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {adding ? (
        <TicketCard className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
          <input value={newLabel} onChange={(e) => setNewLabel(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Home — House 12, Street 4"
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          <select value={newZone} onChange={(e) => setNewZone(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
            {ZONES.map((z) => <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>{z.name} zone</option>)}
          </select>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <GhostButton full onClick={() => setAdding(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
            <PrimaryButton full onClick={saveNewAddress}>Save address</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </TicketCard>
      ) : (
        <button onClick={() => setAdding(true)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-orange-600 font-medium">
          <Plus size={16} /> Add a new address
        </button>
      )}

      <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, step: "review" }))}>
        Continue <ChevronRight size={17} />
      </PrimaryButton>
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerReviewStep({ draft, setDraft, addresses, onOrderCreated }) {
  const [scheduled, setScheduled] = useState(false);
  const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === draft.zone);
  const address = addresses[draft.addressIdx];
  const attachIcon = { photo: Camera, doc: FileText, voice: Mic, video: Package };

  const place = () => {
    const id = "TK-" + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
    onOrderCreated({
      id, type: "customer", zone: draft.zone, status: "placed", riderId: null,
      text: draft.text || "(see attachments)", images: draft.attachments, urgent: draft.urgent,
      notes: draft.notes, address: address?.label, createdAt: Date.now(),
      bill: { amount: 0, uploaded: false }, payment: { method: "cash", status: "pending" }, chat: [],
      scheduled: scheduled || !!draft.scheduledFor,
      scheduledFor: draft.scheduledFor ? `${draft.scheduledDate || "Today"} ${draft.scheduledTime || ""}`.trim() : null,
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <button onClick={() => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, step: "address" }))} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-600">
        <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back to address
      </button>

      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">Review your order</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Confirm everything before placing</p>
      </div>

      <TicketCard className="p-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Order details</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">{draft.text}</p>
        {draft.attachments.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap mt-3">
            {draft.attachments.map((att, i) => {
              const Icon = attachIcon[att.type];
              return <Badge key={i} tone="orange"><Icon size={11} className="inline mr-1 -mt-0.5" />{att.label}</Badge>;
            })}
          </div>
        )}
        {draft.notes && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">Note: {draft.notes}</p>}
        {draft.urgent && <div className="mt-2"><Badge tone="amber">Urgent delivery</Badge></div>}
      </TicketCard>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
        <MapPin size={18} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100">{address?.label}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{zone?.name} zone</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <TicketCard className="p-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Delivery charges (company)</p>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1"><span>Delivery charge</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(zone?.charge || 0)}</span></div>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"><span>Platform fee</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(zone?.platformFee || 0)}</span></div>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">Item cost will be added once your rider purchases and uploads the bill.</p>
      </TicketCard>

      {zone?.status === "paused" && (
        <button onClick={() => setScheduled((s) => !s)}
          className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl border transition ${scheduled ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800"}`}>
          <Clock size={17} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-left flex-1">
            <div className="text-sm font-medium">Schedule for when zone reopens</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{zone?.reason || "Area currently closed"}</div>
          </div>
          <div className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${scheduled ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-500" : "border-stone-300"}`}>
            {scheduled && <Check size={12} className="text-white" />}
          </div>
        </button>
      )}

      <PrimaryButton full onClick={place}>Place Order <ChevronRight size={17} /></PrimaryButton>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChatBox({ chat, onSend, self, deliveredAt, title = "Chat" }) {
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(null); // {type, label, url}
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef(null);
  const chunksRef = useRef([]);
  const scrollRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => { scrollRef.current?.scrollTo(0, scrollRef.current.scrollHeight); }, [chat.length]);

  const closed = deliveredAt ? (Date.now() - deliveredAt) > 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 : false;

  const send = () => {
    if (!msg.trim() && !pending) return;
    onSend({ from: self, type: pending?.type || "text", text: msg, mediaUrl: pending?.url, label: pending?.label, ts: Date.now() });
    setMsg(""); setPending(null);
  };

  const handleFile = (file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const isImage = file.type.startsWith("image");
    setPending({ type: isImage ? "photo" : "doc", label: file.name, url: isImage ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : undefined });
  };

  const startRec = async () => {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      chunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => chunksRef.current.push(e.data);
      recorder.onstop = () => {
        const blob = new Blob(chunksRef.current, { type: "audio/webm" });
        setPending({ type: "voice", label: "Voice message", url: URL.createObjectURL(blob) });
        stream.getTracks().forEach((tr) => tr.stop());
      };
      mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start();
      setRecording(true);
    } catch {
      setPending({ type: "voice", label: "Voice message (simulated)" });
    }
  };
  const stopRec = () => { setRecording(false); mediaRecorderRef.current?.stop(); };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
      <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5"><MessageCircle size={15} /> {title}</p>
      <div ref={scrollRef} className="space-y-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto mb-3">
        {chat.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-4">No messages yet — say hello 👋</p>}
        {chat.map((c, i) => (
          <div key={i} className={`flex ${c.from === self ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}>
            <div className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-xl max-w-[78%] ${c.from === self ? "bg-orange-500 text-white" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200"}`}>
              {c.type === "photo" && c.mediaUrl && (
                <div className="relative group">
                  <img src={c.mediaUrl} alt="" className="rounded-lg max-w-[180px] mb-1" />
                  <a href={c.mediaUrl} download className="absolute top-1 right-1 bg-black/50 rounded p-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"><Copy size={11} className="text-white" /></a>
                </div>
              )}
              {c.type === "doc" && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-black/10 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 mb-1">
                  <FileText size={13} /> <span className="text-xs truncate max-w-[120px]">{c.label}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {c.type === "voice" && c.mediaUrl && <audio src={c.mediaUrl} controls className="h-8 mb-1 max-w-[180px]" />}
              {c.type === "voice" && !c.mediaUrl && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-black/10 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 mb-1"><Mic size={13} /> <span className="text-xs">{c.label}</span></div>
              )}
              {c.text && <p className="text-xs">{c.text}</p>}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {closed ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-2 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg">Chat closed — this order was delivered more than 24 hours ago</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {pending && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
              {pending.type === "photo" && pending.url && <img src={pending.url} className="w-8 h-8 rounded object-cover" alt="" />}
              {pending.type !== "photo" && <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 flex items-center justify-center"><Mic size={13} className="text-orange-500" /></div>}
              <span className="text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 flex-1 truncate">{pending.label}</span>
              <button onClick={() => setPending(null)}><X size={13} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
            </div>
          )}
          {recording && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-red-50 border border-red-100 rounded-lg p-2">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-red-500 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="text-xs text-red-600 flex-1">Recording...</span>
              <button onClick={stopRec} className="text-xs bg-red-500 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded">Stop</button>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <label className="w-9 h-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 cursor-pointer">
              <Camera size={15} />
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,.pdf,.doc,.docx" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { handleFile(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
            </label>
            <button onClick={recording ? stopRec : startRec} className={`w-9 h-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg border flex items-center justify-center ${recording ? "bg-red-500 border-red-500 text-white" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300"}`}>
              <Mic size={15} />
            </button>
            <input value={msg} onChange={(e) => setMsg(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && send()}
              placeholder={pending ? "Add a caption..." : "Type a message..."}
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <button onClick={send} className="w-9 h-9 shrink-0 bg-orange-500 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-white"><Send size={14} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function CopyField({ label, value, copyable = true }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const copy = () => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(value); setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800); };
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</p>
        <p className="font-mono-tk text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 truncate">{value}</p>
      </div>
      {copyable && (
        <button onClick={copy} className="shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-lg border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500">
          {copied ? <Check size={13} className="text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy size={13} />}
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminAccountDisplay({ account }) {
  const a = account || DEFAULT_ADMIN_ACCOUNT;
  return (
    <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2">
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Transfer to Taskly's account:</p>
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <CopyField label="Bank" value={a.bankName} copyable={false} />
        <CopyField label="Account Holder" value={a.accountHolder} copyable={false} />
        <CopyField label="Account Number" value={a.accountNumber} />
        <CopyField label="IBAN" value={a.iban} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function LiveTimer({ startedAt, minutes }) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  const totalSec = minutes * 60;
  const elapsedSec = Math.floor((now - startedAt) / 1000);
  const remainingSec = Math.max(totalSec - elapsedSec, 0);
  const mm = String(Math.floor(remainingSec / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const ss = String(remainingSec % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  const late = remainingSec === 0;
  return (
    <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono-tk text-sm font-semibold ${late ? "text-red-500" : "text-orange-600"}`}>
      <Timer size={14} /> {late ? "Running late" : `${mm}:${ss}`}
    </div>
  );
}

const STATUS_ICONS = { placed: Clock, preparing: Package, packing: FileText, out_for_delivery: Bike, arrived: MapPin, delivered: CheckCircle2 };

function StatusTimeline({ status }) {
  const idx = STATUS_STEPS.indexOf(status);
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start">
      {STATUS_STEPS.map((s, i) => {
        const Icon = STATUS_ICONS[s];
        const done = i < idx;
        const current = i === idx;
        return (
          <React.Fragment key={s}>
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
              <div className={`relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
                done ? "bg-orange-500 text-white" :
                current ? "bg-orange-500 text-white ring-4 ring-orange-100 dark:ring-orange-500/20" :
                "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-500"}`}>
                {done ? <Check size={15} strokeWidth={2.5} /> : <Icon size={14} strokeWidth={2} />}
                {current && <span className="absolute -inset-0.5 rounded-full border-2 border-orange-400 animate-ping opacity-40" />}
              </div>
              <span className={`text-[9px] text-center w-14 leading-tight ${i <= idx ? "text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 font-medium" : "text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-600"}`}>{STATUS_LABEL[s]}</span>
            </div>
            {i < STATUS_STEPS.length - 1 && <div className={`flex-1 h-0.5 mt-4 ${i < idx ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700"}`} />}
          </React.Fragment>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerOrders({ t, orders, setOrders, openOrder, setOpenOrder, wallet, setWallet, riders }) {
  const [view, setView] = useState("current"); // current | completed
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  if (openOrder) {
    const order = orders.find((o) => o.id === openOrder);
    return <CustomerOrderDetail t={t} order={order} onBack={() => setOpenOrder(null)} setOrders={setOrders} wallet={wallet} setWallet={setWallet} />;
  }
  const active = orders.filter((o) => o.status !== "delivered");
  const past = orders.filter((o) => o.status === "delivered");

  const reorder = (source) => {
    const id = "TK-" + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
    setOrders((prev) => [{ ...source, id, status: "placed", riderId: null, createdAt: Date.now(), bill: { amount: 0, uploaded: false }, chat: [], rating: undefined }, ...prev]);
  };

  const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = (view === "current" ? active : past).filter((o) =>
    !q || o.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (o.text || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
  );

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 p-1 rounded-xl">
        <button onClick={() => setView("current")}
          className={`py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition ${view === "current" ? "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-orange-600 shadow-sm" : "text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
          {t.activeOrders} {active.length > 0 && <span className="ml-1">({active.length})</span>}
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setView("completed")}
          className={`py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition ${view === "completed" ? "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-orange-600 shadow-sm" : "text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
          {t.pastOrders} {past.length > 0 && <span className="ml-1">({past.length})</span>}
        </button>
      </div>

      {(active.length + past.length) > 3 && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5">
          <Search size={15} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 shrink-0" />
          <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search by order ID or item..."
            className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm outline-none bg-transparent" />
          {search && <button onClick={() => setSearch("")}><X size={14} className="text-stone-400" /></button>}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">{q ? "No orders match your search" : t.noOrders}</p>}
        {list.map((o) => (
          <OrderRow key={o.id} order={o} onClick={() => setOpenOrder(o.id)} past={view === "completed"}
            onReorder={view === "completed" ? () => reorder(o) : undefined} t={t} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function OrderRow({ order, onClick, past, onReorder, t }) {
  const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === order.zone);
  return (
    <TicketCard className="p-4 hover:shadow-md transition" >
      <div onClick={onClick} className="flex items-start justify-between cursor-pointer">
        <div className="flex-1 pr-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{order.id}</span>
            {order.urgent && <Badge tone="amber">Urgent</Badge>}
            {order.scheduled && <Badge tone="default"><Clock size={10} className="inline mr-0.5 -mt-0.5" />{order.scheduledFor || "Scheduled"}</Badge>}
            {past ? <Badge tone="green">Delivered</Badge> : <Badge tone="orange">{STATUS_LABEL[order.status]}</Badge>}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 line-clamp-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">{order.text}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1.5">{zone?.name} · {timeAgo(order.createdAt)}</p>
        </div>
        <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-stone-300 mt-1" />
      </div>
      {past && onReorder && (
        <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(); }}
          className="mt-3 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-orange-600 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 hover:bg-orange-100 rounded-lg py-2 transition">
          <Plus size={13} /> {t?.reorder || "Reorder"}
        </button>
      )}
    </TicketCard>
  );
}

function CustomerSOSButton() {
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <button onClick={() => setConfirm(true)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 dark:hover:bg-red-500/20 transition">
        <ShieldAlert size={15} />
      </button>
      {confirm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-xs w-full text-center">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
              <ShieldAlert size={18} className="text-red-500" />
            </div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100 mb-1">Send emergency alert?</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-5">Admin and our helpline will be notified immediately with your order and location details.</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setConfirm(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <button onClick={() => { setConfirm(false); setSent(true); setTimeout(() => setSent(false), 4000); }}
                className="w-full px-5 py-3 bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl font-medium transition">
                Send SOS
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {sent && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full px-5 py-3 shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium z-40">
          <ShieldAlert size={15} /> SOS sent — help is on the way
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function CustomerOrderDetail({ t, order, onBack, setOrders, wallet, setWallet }) {
  const [showOnlinePay, setShowOnlinePay] = useState(false);
  const [accCopied, setAccCopied] = useState(null);
  const [screenshotUploaded, setScreenshotUploaded] = useState(false);
  const [showDispute, setShowDispute] = useState(false);
  const [disputeReason, setDisputeReason] = useState("");
  const [showCancelReq, setShowCancelReq] = useState(false);
  const [cancelReason, setCancelReason] = useState("");
  const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === order.zone);
  const rider = RIDERS.find((r) => r.id === order.riderId);
  const total = order.bill.total ?? (order.bill.amount + (order.bill.amount > 0 ? zone.charge + zone.platformFee : 0));
  const attachIcon = { photo: Camera, doc: FileText, voice: Mic, video: Package };

  const submitDispute = () => {
    if (!disputeReason.trim()) return;
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, disputeStatus: "open", disputeReason } : o));
    setShowDispute(false); setDisputeReason("");
  };

  const submitCancelRequest = () => {
    if (!cancelReason.trim()) return;
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, cancelRequest: { reason: cancelReason, status: "pending", requestedAt: Date.now() } } : o));
    setShowCancelReq(false); setCancelReason("");
  };

  const advance = () => {
    const idx = STATUS_STEPS.indexOf(order.status);
    const next = STATUS_STEPS[Math.min(idx + 1, STATUS_STEPS.length - 1)];
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? {
      ...o, status: next,
      bill: next === "packing" ? { amount: 780, uploaded: true, deliveryCharge: zone.charge, platformFee: zone.platformFee, total: 780 + zone.charge + zone.platformFee } : o.bill,
      deliveredAt: next === "delivered" ? Date.now() : o.deliveredAt,
    } : o));
  };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <button onClick={onBack} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-600">
        <ChevronLeft size={16} /> {t.orders}
      </button>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="font-mono-tk text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{order.id}</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {order.status !== "delivered" && rider && <CustomerSOSButton />}
          {order.status !== "delivered" && <LiveTimer startedAt={order.createdAt} minutes={zone.timer} />}
        </div>
      </div>

      <TicketCard className="p-5">
        <StatusTimeline status={order.status} />
        {order.status !== "delivered" && (
          <button onClick={advance} className="text-xs text-orange-500 underline mt-4">simulate next status (demo)</button>
        )}
      </TicketCard>

      {order.cancelRequest && (
        <div className={`rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-2 ${order.cancelRequest.status === "pending" ? "bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100" : order.cancelRequest.status === "approved" ? "bg-red-50 border border-red-100" : "bg-stone-50 border border-stone-100"}`}>
          <AlertCircle size={15} className={order.cancelRequest.status === "pending" ? "text-amber-600" : order.cancelRequest.status === "approved" ? "text-red-500" : "text-stone-400"} />
          <p className="text-xs">
            {order.cancelRequest.status === "pending" && "Cancellation requested — waiting for Admin review"}
            {order.cancelRequest.status === "approved" && "Order cancelled by Admin"}
            {order.cancelRequest.status === "rejected" && `Cancellation request declined: ${order.cancelRequest.adminNote || "no reason given"}`}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
      {!order.cancelRequest && order.status !== "delivered" && order.status !== "out_for_delivery" && order.status !== "arrived" && (
        <button onClick={() => setShowCancelReq((s) => !s)} className="text-xs text-red-500 underline">Request order cancellation</button>
      )}
      {showCancelReq && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-100 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2">
          <p className="text-xs text-red-700">You can't cancel directly — Admin reviews every request since a rider may already be shopping for this order.</p>
          <textarea value={cancelReason} onChange={(e) => setCancelReason(e.target.value)} rows={2} placeholder="Why do you want to cancel?"
            className="w-full border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none focus:border-red-400 bg-white" />
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setShowCancelReq(false)}>Back</GhostButton>
            <PrimaryButton small full onClick={submitCancelRequest} disabled={!cancelReason.trim()}>Send Request</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Full order details — text + every attachment, exactly as the customer sent them */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Order details</p>
        {order.text && <p className="text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">{order.text}</p>}
        {order.notes && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">Note: {order.notes}</p>}

        {order.images && order.images.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
            {order.images.map((att, i) => {
              const Icon = attachIcon[att.type] || FileText;
              return (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-xl p-2.5">
                  {att.previewUrl ? (
                    <img src={att.previewUrl} alt="" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg object-cover shrink-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-600" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Icon size={17} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 truncate">{att.label}</p>
                    {att.audioUrl && <audio src={att.audioUrl} controls className="w-full h-8 mt-1" />}
                    {att.caption && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">"{att.caption}"</p>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {order.address && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
            <MapPin size={13} /> {order.address}
          </div>
        )}

        {rider && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 text-xs font-semibold">
              {rider.name.split(" ").map(w => w[0]).join("")}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">{rider.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Star size={11} className="fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" /> {rider.rating}</p>
            </div>
            <Badge tone="green">Assigned</Badge>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {order.bill.uploaded && (
        <TicketCard className="p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Bill / Invoice</p>
            {order.disputeStatus ? (
              <Badge tone="amber">Dispute {order.disputeStatus}</Badge>
            ) : (
              <button onClick={() => setShowDispute((s) => !s)} className="text-xs text-red-500 underline">{t.disputeBill}</button>
            )}
          </div>
          {showDispute && (
            <div className="mb-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-100 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2">
              <textarea value={disputeReason} onChange={(e) => setDisputeReason(e.target.value)} rows={2}
                placeholder="What looks wrong with this bill?"
                className="w-full border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none focus:border-red-400 bg-white" />
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setShowDispute(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
                <PrimaryButton small full onClick={submitDispute} disabled={!disputeReason.trim()}>Submit Dispute</PrimaryButton>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {order.bill.invoicePhotos?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap mb-3">
              {order.bill.invoicePhotos.map((url, i) => (
                <img key={i} src={url} alt="" className="w-14 h-14 rounded-lg object-cover border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700" />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Items purchased</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.amount)}</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Delivery charge ({zone.name})</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.deliveryCharge ?? zone.charge)}</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Platform fee</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.platformFee ?? zone.platformFee)}</span></div>
            <div className="border-t border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 my-2" />
            <div className="flex justify-between font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100"><span>Total</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(total)}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mt-4">
            <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setShowOnlinePay((s) => !s)}>{t.cash}</GhostButton>
            <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => setShowOnlinePay((s) => !s)}>{t.online}</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
          {showOnlinePay && (
            <div className="mt-3 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2.5">
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Transfer to rider's account:</p>
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Account holder</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{rider?.account?.holderName || rider?.name || "Rider"} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-stone-400">· {rider?.account?.bank || "JazzCash"}</span></p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Account number</p>
                  <p className="font-mono-tk text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{rider?.account?.number || "0300-1234567"}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(rider?.account?.number || "0300-1234567"); setAccCopied("number"); setTimeout(() => setAccCopied(null), 2000); }}
                  className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0"><Copy size={12} /> {accCopied === "number" ? "Copied!" : "Copy"}</button>
              </div>
              {rider?.account?.iban && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide">IBAN</p>
                    <p className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{rider.account.iban}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(rider.account.iban); setAccCopied("iban"); setTimeout(() => setAccCopied(null), 2000); }}
                    className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0"><Copy size={12} /> {accCopied === "iban" ? "Copied!" : "Copy"}</button>
                </div>
              )}
              {screenshotUploaded ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-xs text-emerald-700">Screenshot sent — waiting for rider to confirm receipt</span>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) { setScreenshotUploaded(true); setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, paymentProof: { url: URL.createObjectURL(e.target.files[0]), status: "pending" } } : o)); } }} />
                  <Camera size={13} /> <span className="underline">Upload payment screenshot</span>
                </label>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </TicketCard>
      )}

      {rider && (
        <ChatBox chat={order.chat} self="customer" title={t.chat}
          deliveredAt={order.status === "delivered" ? (order.deliveredAt || order.createdAt) : null}
          onSend={(m) => setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, chat: [...o.chat, m] } : o))} />
      )}

      {order.status === "delivered" && !order.rating && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">{t.rateRider}</p>
          <div className="flex justify-center gap-1">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
              <button key={n} onClick={() => setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, rating: n } : o))}>
                <Star size={26} className="text-stone-200 hover:text-amber-400 hover:fill-amber-400 transition" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {order.status === "delivered" && order.rating && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 text-center text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">
          You rated this order {order.rating} <Star size={12} className="inline fill-amber-400 text-amber-400 -mt-0.5" />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerWallet({ t, wallet, setWallet, orders, adminAccount }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [showTopup, setShowTopup] = useState(false);
  const [topupAmount, setTopupAmount] = useState("");
  const [screenshot, setScreenshot] = useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const referralCode = "SANA-TK294";
  const loyaltyPoints = 320;
  const tier = loyaltyPoints >= 500 ? "Gold" : loyaltyPoints >= 200 ? "Silver" : "Bronze";
  const tierColor = { Gold: "text-amber-500", Silver: "text-stone-400", Bronze: "text-orange-700" };
  const nextTierAt = tier === "Gold" ? null : tier === "Silver" ? 500 : 200;

  const copyCode = () => {
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };
  const submitTopup = () => {
    setSubmitted(true); setShowTopup(false); setTopupAmount(""); setScreenshot(false);
    setTimeout(() => setSubmitted(false), 4000);
  };
  const positive = wallet >= 0;
  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <TicketCard className="p-6 text-center">
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1">{t.walletBalance}</p>
        <p className={`font-display text-3xl font-bold font-mono-tk ${positive ? "text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100" : "text-red-500"}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(wallet))}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">{positive ? "Credit — applied to next order" : "Due — will be added to your next bill"}</p>
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowTopup((s) => !s)} className="mt-4 mx-auto"><Plus size={14} /> Top Up</PrimaryButton>

        {showTopup && (
          <div className="mt-4 text-left space-y-3">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {[500, 1000, 2000].map((v) => (
                <button key={v} onClick={() => setTopupAmount(String(v))}
                  className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg py-2 text-sm font-mono-tk hover:border-orange-300">{fmt(v)}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <input value={topupAmount} onChange={(e) => setTopupAmount(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="Custom amount"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <AdminAccountDisplay account={adminAccount} />
            {screenshot ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-lg p-2.5">
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                <span className="text-xs text-emerald-700">Screenshot attached</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300">
                <Camera size={14} /> Upload payment screenshot
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) setScreenshot(true); }} />
              </label>
            )}
            <PrimaryButton small full onClick={submitTopup} disabled={!topupAmount || !screenshot}>Submit for Approval</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        )}
        {submitted && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 mt-3">✓ Sent — your wallet will update once Admin approves</p>}
      </TicketCard>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Award size={22} className={tierColor[tier]} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <div className="flex-1">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">{tier} Member</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{loyaltyPoints} points {nextTierAt && `· ${nextTierAt - loyaltyPoints} more to reach next tier`}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Gift size={20} className="text-orange-500" strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <div className="flex-1">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-800">Refer a friend</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-orange-600">Both get Rs. 100 off · code: <span className="font-mono-tk">{referralCode}</span></p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={copyCode} className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
          <Copy size={12} /> {copied ? "Copied!" : "Copy"}
        </button>
      </div>

      <SavedPaymentMethods />

      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-2">Wallet history</p>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center"><ArrowDownRight size={14} className="text-red-500" /></div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm">Order TK-1039 adjustment</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">You paid Rs. 150 less</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="text-sm font-mono-tk text-red-500">-150</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SavedPaymentMethods() {
  const [methods, setMethods] = useState([{ id: 1, provider: "JazzCash", number: "0300-1234567" }]);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("JazzCash");
  const [number, setNumber] = useState("");

  const save = () => {
    if (!number.trim()) return;
    setMethods((prev) => [...prev, { id: Date.now(), provider, number }]);
    setNumber(""); setAdding(false);
  };
  const remove = (id) => setMethods((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id));

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Saved Payment Methods</p>
        <button onClick={() => setAdding((a) => !a)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 flex items-center gap-1 font-medium"><Plus size={13} /> Add</button>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {methods.map((m) => (
          <div key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Wallet size={16} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">{m.provider}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-mono-tk">{m.number}</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => remove(m.id)} className="text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500"><X size={14} /></button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {methods.length === 0 && !adding && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-3">No saved payment methods yet</p>}
        {adding && (
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2">
            <select value={provider} onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
              <option>JazzCash</option><option>Easypaisa</option><option>Bank Account</option>
            </select>
            <input value={number} onChange={(e) => setNumber(e.target.value)} placeholder="Account / wallet number"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setAdding(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton small full onClick={save}>Save</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PromoApply() {
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [applied, setApplied] = useState(null);
  const apply = () => {
    if (!code.trim()) return;
    const valid = ["FIRST20", "TASKLY100"].includes(code.toUpperCase());
    setApplied(valid ? { ok: true, code: code.toUpperCase() } : { ok: false });
  };
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)} placeholder="Enter promo code"
          className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={apply}>Apply</PrimaryButton>
      </div>
      {applied && (
        <p className={`text-xs mt-2 ${applied.ok ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-500"}`}>
          {applied.ok ? `✓ ${applied.code} applied — discount added to your next order` : "Invalid or expired code"}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const SERVICE_CATEGORIES = [
  { id: "plumber", name: "Plumber", icon: Timer },
  { id: "electrician", name: "Electrician", icon: Zap },
  { id: "ac", name: "AC Technician", icon: PauseCircle },
  { id: "carpenter", name: "Carpenter", icon: Package },
  { id: "painter", name: "Painter", icon: ImageIcon },
  { id: "cleaning", name: "Home Cleaning", icon: CheckCircle2 },
  { id: "pest", name: "Pest Control", icon: ShieldAlert },
  { id: "cctv", name: "CCTV Technician", icon: Search },
  { id: "mechanic", name: "Mechanic", icon: Settings },
];
const SERVICE_PROVIDERS = {
  plumber: [
    { id: "sp1", name: "Imran Plumbing Works", rating: 4.7, jobs: 340, experience: "8 years", area: "Gulberg, Model Town", charges: "Rs. 500 visit + parts", availability: "Available today" },
    { id: "sp2", name: "Aslam & Sons Plumbers", rating: 4.5, jobs: 210, experience: "12 years", area: "DHA, Cantt", charges: "Rs. 600 visit + parts", availability: "Available tomorrow" },
  ],
  electrician: [
    { id: "sp3", name: "Bright Spark Electricals", rating: 4.8, jobs: 410, experience: "6 years", area: "Johar Town, Wapda Town", charges: "Rs. 450 visit + parts", availability: "Available today" },
    { id: "sp4", name: "Kashif Electric Services", rating: 4.6, jobs: 190, experience: "9 years", area: "Gulberg, Garden Town", charges: "Rs. 500 visit + parts", availability: "Available today" },
  ],
  ac: [{ id: "sp5", name: "CoolTech AC Services", rating: 4.7, jobs: 280, experience: "7 years", area: "All areas", charges: "Rs. 800 service", availability: "Available today" }],
  carpenter: [{ id: "sp6", name: "Wood Craft Carpentry", rating: 4.6, jobs: 150, experience: "10 years", area: "Gulberg, DHA", charges: "Rs. 700 visit + work", availability: "Available in 2 days" }],
  painter: [{ id: "sp7", name: "Perfect Finish Painters", rating: 4.5, jobs: 175, experience: "5 years", area: "All areas", charges: "Rs. 40/sq.ft", availability: "Available this week" }],

  pest: [{ id: "sp9", name: "SafeHome Pest Control", rating: 4.6, jobs: 230, experience: "6 years", area: "All areas", charges: "Rs. 2000 treatment", availability: "Available tomorrow" }],
  cctv: [{ id: "sp10", name: "SecureView CCTV", rating: 4.7, jobs: 160, experience: "5 years", area: "All areas", charges: "Rs. 1500 setup + camera cost", availability: "Available in 2 days" }],
  mechanic: [{ id: "sp11", name: "AutoFix Mobile Mechanic", rating: 4.5, jobs: 300, experience: "9 years", area: "All areas", charges: "Rs. 600 visit + parts", availability: "Available today" }],
};
const SERVICE_FEE = 150;

function HomeServicesView({ onClose, inline }) {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(null);
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(null);
  const [request, setRequest] = useState("");
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const submitRequest = () => {
    if (!request.trim()) return;
    setSent(true);
  };

  const content = (
    <>
      {!inline && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
          <button onClick={() => { if (provider) setProvider(null); else if (category) setCategory(null); else onClose(); }}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">
            <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back
          </button>
          <button onClick={onClose}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
        </div>
      )}
      {inline && (category || provider) && (
        <button onClick={() => { if (provider) setProvider(null); else setCategory(null); }}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-4">
          <ChevronLeft size={16} /> {provider ? "All providers" : "All categories"}
        </button>
      )}

      {!category && (
        <>
          <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">Home Services</h2>
