          <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-5">Trusted, vetted professionals — request a visit, provider responds directly. Rs. {SERVICE_FEE} platform fee applies per confirmed job.</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {SERVICE_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
              <button key={c.id} onClick={() => setCategory(c.id)} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-3.5 text-center hover:border-orange-300 transition">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto rounded-xl bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-2"><c.icon size={17} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium leading-tight">{c.name}</p>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {category && !provider && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-3">{SERVICE_CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === category)?.name}s near you</h2>
          {(SERVICE_PROVIDERS[category] || []).length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No providers available in this category yet</p>}
          {(SERVICE_PROVIDERS[category] || []).map((p) => (
            <button key={p.id} onClick={() => setProvider(p)} className="w-full text-left bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 font-semibold shrink-0">{p.name.split(" ").map(w=>w[0]).join("").slice(0,2)}</div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{p.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Star size={11} className="fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" /> {p.rating} · {p.jobs} jobs · {p.experience}</p>
                </div>
                <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-stone-300 shrink-0" />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 pt-2 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><MapPin size={11} /> {p.area}</div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><Wallet size={11} /> {p.charges}</div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 col-span-2"><Clock size={11} className="text-emerald-500" /> <span className="text-emerald-600">{p.availability}</span></div>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {provider && !sent && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <TicketCard className="p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 font-semibold shrink-0">{provider.name.split(" ").map(w=>w[0]).join("").slice(0,2)}</div>
              <div>
                <p className="font-semibold">{provider.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Star size={11} className="fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" /> {provider.rating} · {provider.jobs} jobs · {provider.experience}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 pt-2 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><MapPin size={11} /> {provider.area}</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><Wallet size={11} /> {provider.charges}</div>
            </div>
          </TicketCard>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Describe what you need</label>
            <textarea value={request} onChange={(e) => setRequest(e.target.value)} rows={4}
              placeholder="e.g. Kitchen tap is leaking, need it fixed today evening"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />

            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 mt-2.5">
              <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 transition cursor-pointer">
                <Camera size={16} /><span className="text-[10px]">Camera</span>
                <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => handleSPFile(e.target.files?.[0])} />
              </label>
              <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 transition cursor-pointer">
                <ImageIcon size={16} /><span className="text-[10px]">Gallery</span>
                <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => handleSPFile(e.target.files?.[0])} />
              </label>
              <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600 transition cursor-pointer">
                <FileText size={16} /><span className="text-[10px]">Document</span>
                <input type="file" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => handleSPFile(e.target.files?.[0], "doc")} />
              </label>
              <button type="button" onClick={spRecording ? stopSPRecording : startSPRecording}
                className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border transition ${spRecording ? "bg-red-500 border-red-500 text-white" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-600"}`}>
                <Mic size={16} /><span className="text-[10px]">{spRecording ? "Stop" : "Voice"}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {spAttachments.length > 0 && (
              <div className="space-y-2 mt-2.5">
                {spAttachments.map((att, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
                    {att.previewUrl ? <img src={att.previewUrl} alt="" className="w-9 h-9 rounded object-cover" /> :
                      <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 flex items-center justify-center">{att.type === "voice" ? <Mic size={14} className="text-orange-500" /> : <FileText size={14} className="text-orange-500" />}</div>}
                    <span className="text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 flex-1 truncate">{att.label}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => setSpAttachments((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))}><X size={13} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-orange-700">
            Platform fee: Rs. {SERVICE_FEE} — the provider's own service charge is agreed directly with you once they respond.
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton full disabled={!request.trim() && spAttachments.length === 0} onClick={submitRequest}>Send Request</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}

      {sent && (
        <div className="text-center py-16">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4"><CheckCircle2 size={26} className="text-emerald-500" /></div>
          <p className="font-semibold mb-1">Request sent to {provider.name}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-6">They'll respond shortly to confirm timing and their charges</p>
          {!inline ? <PrimaryButton onClick={onClose}>Done</PrimaryButton> : <PrimaryButton onClick={() => { setSent(false); setProvider(null); setCategory(null); }}>Browse more services</PrimaryButton>}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );

  if (inline) return <div className="px-5 py-5">{content}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-[#FAF9F6] dark:bg-stone-900 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen px-5 py-5">{content}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CustomerProfile({ t, favShops, profile, setProfile, addresses, setAddresses }) {
  const [openPanel, setOpenPanel] = useState(null); // 'promos' | 'help' | 'terms'
  const [editingProfile, setEditingProfile] = useState(false);
  const [profileDraft, setProfileDraft] = useState(profile);
  const [profileSaved, setProfileSaved] = useState(false);

  const ADDRESS_TYPES = [
    { key: "Home", icon: Home },
    { key: "Office", icon: Briefcase },
    { key: "Other", icon: MapPin },
  ];
  const [addressModal, setAddressModal] = useState(null); // null | 'new' | address object being edited
  const [addrType, setAddrType] = useState("Home");
  const [addrLabel, setAddrLabel] = useState("");
  const [addrText, setAddrText] = useState("");
  const [addrZone, setAddrZone] = useState(ZONES[0].id);
  const [confirmDeleteId, setConfirmDeleteId] = useState(null);
  const [complaint, setComplaint] = useState("");
  const [complaintSent, setComplaintSent] = useState(false);
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([
    { id: "TCK-8841", text: "Rider was late by 20 minutes on my last order", status: "resolved" },
  ]);

  const submitTicket = () => {
    if (!complaint.trim()) return;
    setTickets((prev) => [{ id: "TCK-" + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000), text: complaint, status: "open" }, ...prev]);
    setComplaint("");
    setComplaintSent(true); setTimeout(() => setComplaintSent(false), 3000);
  };

  const startEditProfile = () => { setProfileDraft(profile); setEditingProfile(true); };
  const saveProfile = () => {
    if (!profileDraft.name.trim() || !profileDraft.phone.trim()) return;
    setProfile(profileDraft);
    setEditingProfile(false);
    setProfileSaved(true); setTimeout(() => setProfileSaved(false), 2500);
  };

  const openNewAddress = () => { setAddrType("Home"); setAddrLabel(""); setAddrText(""); setAddrZone(ZONES[0].id); setAddressModal("new"); };
  const openEditAddress = (a) => { setAddrType(a.type); setAddrLabel(a.label); setAddrText(a.address); setAddrZone(a.zone || ZONES[0].id); setAddressModal(a); };
  const saveAddress = () => {
    if (!addrText.trim()) return;
    if (addressModal === "new") {
      setAddresses((prev) => [...prev, { id: Date.now(), type: addrType, label: addrLabel, address: addrText, zone: addrZone }]);
    } else {
      setAddresses((prev) => prev.map((a) => a.id === addressModal.id ? { ...a, type: addrType, label: addrLabel, address: addrText, zone: addrZone } : a));
    }
    setAddressModal(null);
  };
  const deleteAddress = (id) => { setAddresses((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== id)); setConfirmDeleteId(null); };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        {!editingProfile ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 font-semibold shrink-0">
              {profile.name.split(" ").map(w => w[0]).join("").slice(0,2)}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="font-medium truncate">{profile.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-mono-tk">{profile.phone}</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={startEditProfile} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-orange-600 border border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 shrink-0 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition">
              <Pencil size={12} /> Edit
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">Edit Profile</p>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Full name</label>
              <input value={profileDraft.name} onChange={(e) => setProfileDraft((p) => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))}
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Contact number</label>
              <input value={profileDraft.phone} onChange={(e) => setProfileDraft((p) => ({ ...p, phone: e.target.value }))}
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
              <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setEditingProfile(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton small full onClick={saveProfile} disabled={!profileDraft.name.trim() || !profileDraft.phone.trim()}>Save Changes</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {profileSaved && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 text-center mt-2">✓ Profile updated</p>}
      </div>

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Saved Addresses</p>
          <button onClick={openNewAddress} className="text-xs text-orange-600 flex items-center gap-1 font-medium"><Plus size={13} /> Add</button>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {addresses.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-4">No saved addresses yet</p>}
          {addresses.map((a) => {
            const TypeIcon = ADDRESS_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === a.type)?.icon || MapPin;
            return (
              <div key={a.id} className="flex items-start gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><TypeIcon size={16} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{a.type}{a.label && <span className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-normal"> · {a.label}</span>}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mt-0.5">{a.address}</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-orange-500 mt-0.5">{ZONES.find(z => z.id === a.zone)?.name || "Unassigned area"}, {CITY}</p>
                  {confirmDeleteId === a.id && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 border border-red-100 dark:border-red-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
                      <span className="text-xs text-red-600 flex-1">Delete this address?</span>
                      <button onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteId(null)} className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 px-2">Cancel</button>
                      <button onClick={() => deleteAddress(a.id)} className="text-xs bg-red-500 text-white rounded-md px-2.5 py-1">Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                  <button onClick={() => openEditAddress(a)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition"><Pencil size={13} /></button>
                  <button onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteId(a.id)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-500/10 transition"><Trash2 size={13} /></button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {addressModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-end justify-center">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <p className="font-display font-bold">{addressModal === "new" ? "Add New Address" : "Edit Address"}</p>
              <button onClick={() => setAddressModal(null)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1.5 block">Address type</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {ADDRESS_TYPES.map((tp) => (
                  <button key={tp.key} onClick={() => setAddrType(tp.key)}
                    className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border transition ${addrType === tp.key ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
                    <tp.icon size={16} />
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium">{tp.key}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Label <span className="font-normal">(optional, e.g. "Mother's House")</span></label>
              <input value={addrLabel} onChange={(e) => setAddrLabel(e.target.value)} placeholder="Optional nickname"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Full address</label>
              <textarea value={addrText} onChange={(e) => setAddrText(e.target.value)} rows={2} placeholder="House / street / area details"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Area (determines delivery charge)</label>
              <select value={addrZone} onChange={(e) => setAddrZone(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
                {ZONES.map((z) => <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>{z.name}, {CITY} — {fmt(z.charge)} delivery</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setAddressModal(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={saveAddress} disabled={!addrText.trim()}>Save Address</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-2">Favorite Shops</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
          {favShops.map((s) => <Badge key={s} tone="orange">{s}</Badge>)}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {[["Promo Codes", "promos"], ["Help & Support", "help"], ["Terms & Privacy Policy", "terms"]].map(([label, key]) => (
          <button key={key} onClick={() => setOpenPanel(openPanel === key ? null : key)}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
            {label} <ChevronRight size={15} className={`text-stone-300 transition ${openPanel === key ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {openPanel === "promos" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2">
          <PromoApply />
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap pt-2">
            <Badge tone="orange">FIRST20 — 20% off first order</Badge>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {openPanel === "help" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">Need help with an order or have a complaint?</p>
          <textarea value={complaint} onChange={(e) => setComplaint(e.target.value)} rows={3} placeholder="Describe your issue..."
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          <PrimaryButton small full disabled={!complaint.trim()} onClick={submitTicket}>
            Submit Complaint
          </PrimaryButton>
          {complaintSent && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 text-center">✓ Ticket created — our team will get back to you within 24 hours</p>}
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center">Or call our helpline: 0800-TASKLY</p>

          {tickets.length > 0 && (
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Your support tickets</p>
              {tickets.map((tk) => (
                <div key={tk.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg p-2.5">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-mono-tk text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{tk.id}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 truncate">{tk.text}</p>
                  </div>
                  <Badge tone={tk.status === "resolved" ? "green" : tk.status === "in_progress" ? "amber" : "default"}>
                    {tk.status === "resolved" ? "Resolved" : tk.status === "in_progress" ? "In Progress" : "Open"}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {openPanel === "terms" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 leading-relaxed max-h-72 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">1. How orders work</p>
            <p>Taskly riders purchase items on your behalf at prevailing market prices, which vary by shop and are outside our control. You'll always see an itemized bill with proof of purchase before paying.</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">2. Delivery timing</p>
            <p>The timer shown for your area is an estimate, not a guarantee. Traffic, weather, and item availability can affect delivery time.</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">3. Payments & wallet</p>
            <p>You may pay cash on delivery or online. If you ever pay more or less than the final bill, the difference is settled automatically to your wallet and applied to your next order.</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">4. Cancellations</p>
            <p>Orders can only be cancelled by Taskly support once placed, to protect riders who may already be shopping on your behalf.</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">5. Privacy</p>
            <p>Your phone number is never shared with riders directly. Your name and delivery address are only visible to the rider assigned to your active order.</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-1">6. Disputes</p>
            <p>If a bill looks incorrect, use "Dispute this Bill" on the order — our team reviews every dispute within 24 hours.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   SHOPKEEPER APP
   ============================================================ */
function ShopkeeperWalletCard({ wallet, setWallet, adminAccount }) {
  const [showTopup, setShowTopup] = useState(false);
  const [screenshot, setScreenshot] = useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [copiedAcc, setCopiedAcc] = useState(false);
  const positive = wallet >= 0;

  return (
    <TicketCard className="p-5 text-center">
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1">Shop Wallet</p>
      <p className={`font-display text-3xl font-bold font-mono-tk ${positive ? "text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100" : "text-red-500"}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(wallet))}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">{positive ? "Credit" : "You owe Taskly — top up to clear"}</p>
      {!positive && (
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowTopup((s) => !s)} className="mt-4 mx-auto">Top Up</PrimaryButton>
      )}
      {showTopup && (
        <div className="mt-4 text-left space-y-2">
          <AdminAccountDisplay account={adminAccount} />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300">
            <Camera size={14} /> {screenshot ? "Screenshot attached ✓" : "Upload payment screenshot"}
            <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={() => setScreenshot(true)} />
          </label>
          <PrimaryButton small full disabled={!screenshot} onClick={() => { setSubmitted(true); setShowTopup(false); setScreenshot(false); }}>
            Submit for Approval
          </PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}
      {submitted && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 mt-3">✓ Submitted — Admin will approve shortly</p>}
    </TicketCard>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperStore({ products, setProducts, status, setStatus }) {
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState(""); const [price, setPrice] = useState(""); const [qty, setQty] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState(null);

  const addProduct = () => {
    if (!name || !price) return;
    setProducts((prev) => [...prev, { id: Date.now(), name, price: Number(price), qty: qty || "In stock", category: category || "General", photo }]);
    setName(""); setPrice(""); setQty(""); setCategory(""); setPhoto(null); setShowForm(false);
  };
  const removeProduct = (id) => setProducts((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
  const [editingProduct, setEditingProduct] = useState(null);

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-orange-800">Store status</p>
          <Badge tone={status === "live" ? "green" : status === "pending" ? "amber" : status === "rejected" ? "red" : "default"}>
            {status === "live" ? "Live for customers" : status === "pending" ? "Pending Admin approval" : status === "rejected" ? "Rejected — edit & resubmit" : "Draft"}
          </Badge>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-orange-700">Add your products below, then submit for approval. Once approved, customers can order directly from your store on the Taskly app.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Products ({products.length})</p>
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowForm((s) => !s)}><Plus size={13} /> Add Product</PrimaryButton>
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <TicketCard className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
          <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Product name"
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <input value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="Price (Rs.)"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <input value={qty} onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)} placeholder="Quantity / unit"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <input value={category} onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)} placeholder="Menu category (e.g. Tablets, Syrup, Bread)"
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300">
            <Camera size={14} /> {photo ? "Photo attached ✓" : "Add product photo"}
            <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if (f) setPhoto(URL.createObjectURL(f)); }} />
          </label>
          <PrimaryButton small full onClick={addProduct}>Add to Store</PrimaryButton>
        </TicketCard>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        {products.map((p) => (
          <div key={p.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
            {p.photo ? <img src={p.photo} alt="" className="w-full h-20 object-cover" /> : <div className="w-full h-20 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center"><ImageIcon size={20} className="text-stone-300" /></div>}
            <div className="p-2.5">
              <p className="text-xs font-medium truncate">{p.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono-tk text-orange-600">{fmt(p.price)}</p>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1.5">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 truncate">{p.qty}</span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                  <button onClick={() => setEditingProduct(p)}><Settings size={12} className="text-stone-400 hover:text-orange-500" /></button>
                  <button onClick={() => removeProduct(p.id)}><X size={12} className="text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500" /></button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {editingProduct && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <p className="font-display font-bold">Edit Product</p>
              <button onClick={() => setEditingProduct(null)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
            </div>
            <input value={editingProduct.name} onChange={(e) => setEditingProduct((p) => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Product name"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <input value={editingProduct.price} onChange={(e) => setEditingProduct((p) => ({ ...p, price: e.target.value }))} type="number" placeholder="Price"
                className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              <input value={editingProduct.qty} onChange={(e) => setEditingProduct((p) => ({ ...p, qty: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Quantity"
                className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <input value={editingProduct.category} onChange={(e) => setEditingProduct((p) => ({ ...p, category: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Category"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setEditingProduct(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => {
                setProducts((prev) => prev.map((pr) => pr.id === editingProduct.id ? { ...editingProduct, price: Number(editingProduct.price) } : pr));
                setEditingProduct(null);
              }}>Save Changes</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {products.length > 0 && status !== "live" && status !== "pending" && (
        <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => setStatus("pending")}>Submit Store for Approval</PrimaryButton>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ServiceProviderApp({ t, lang, setLang, onExit, dark, setDark, adminAccount, providers, setProviders }) {
  const [myId] = useState(() => "prov-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8));
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("profile");
  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState(0);
  const me = providers.find((p) => p.id === myId);

  const saveProfile = (data) => {
    setProviders((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.find((p) => p.id === myId);
      if (exists) return prev.map((p) => p.id === myId ? { ...p, ...data } : p);
      return [...prev, { id: myId, ...data, status: "pending", jobs: 0, rating: null }];
    });
  };
  const submitForApproval = () => setProviders((prev) => prev.map((p) => p.id === myId ? { ...p, status: "pending" } : p));

  const tabs = [
    { key: "profile", label: "Profile", icon: User },
    { key: "requests", label: "Requests", icon: MessageCircle },
    { key: "wallet", label: "Wallet", icon: Wallet },
  ];

  const [requests, setRequests] = useState([
    { id: "SR-1", customer: "Sana Ahmed", area: "Gulberg", text: "Kitchen tap is leaking, need it fixed today evening", time: "12m ago", status: "new" },
    { id: "SR-2", customer: "Hassan Raza", area: "DHA", text: "Bathroom geyser not heating water", time: "1h ago", status: "new" },
  ]);
  const [completeModal, setCompleteModal] = useState(null);
  const [earned, setEarned] = useState("");

  const respond = (id, accept) => setRequests((prev) => prev.map((r) => r.id === id ? { ...r, status: accept ? "accepted" : "declined" } : r));
  const completeJob = () => {
    const amt = Number(earned) || 0;
    setWallet((w) => w + amt - SERVICE_FEE);
    setRequests((prev) => prev.map((r) => r.id === completeModal ? { ...r, status: "completed", earned: amt } : r));
    setProviders((prev) => prev.map((p) => p.id === myId ? { ...p, jobs: (p.jobs || 0) + 1 } : p));
    setCompleteModal(null); setEarned("");
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen relative pb-24">
      <MobileTopBar title="Service Provider" lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={onExit} dark={dark} setDark={setDark}
        subtitle={me ? me.name : "Set up your profile"} />

      {tab === "profile" && (
        <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
          {me && (
            <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-orange-800">Profile status</p>
              <Badge tone={me.status === "live" ? "green" : me.status === "pending" ? "amber" : me.status === "suspended" ? "red" : "default"}>
                {me.status === "live" ? "Live for customers" : me.status === "pending" ? "Pending Admin approval" : me.status === "suspended" ? "Suspended" : me.status}
              </Badge>
            </div>
          )}
          {me?.status === "live" && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <StatCard label="Completed Jobs" value={String(me.jobs || 0)} icon={CheckCircle2} />
              <StatCard label="Wallet Balance" value={fmt(wallet)} icon={Wallet} />
            </div>
          )}
          <ServiceProviderProfileForm initial={me} onSave={saveProfile} onSubmit={submitForApproval} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "requests" && (
        <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-3">
          {me?.status !== "live" ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">Your profile needs to be approved and live before customers can send requests</p>
          ) : (
            requests.map((r) => (
              <TicketCard key={r.id} className="p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{r.customer} <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-normal">· {r.area}</span></p>
                  {r.status !== "new" && <Badge tone={r.status === "accepted" ? "orange" : r.status === "completed" ? "green" : "default"}>{r.status}</Badge>}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mt-1">{r.text}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">{r.time}</p>
                {r.status === "new" && (
                  <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                    <GhostButton small full onClick={() => respond(r.id, false)}>Decline</GhostButton>
                    <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => respond(r.id, true)}>Accept & Reply</PrimaryButton>
                  </div>
                )}
                {r.status === "accepted" && (
                  <PrimaryButton small full className="mt-3" onClick={() => { setCompleteModal(r.id); setEarned(""); }}>Mark Job Complete</PrimaryButton>
                )}
                {r.status === "completed" && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 mt-2">Job completed — earned {fmt(r.earned)}</p>
                )}
              </TicketCard>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "wallet" && (
        <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
          <TicketCard className="p-6 text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1">Wallet balance</p>
            <p className="font-display text-3xl font-bold font-mono-tk">{fmt(wallet)}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">Rs. {SERVICE_FEE} platform fee is deducted per confirmed job</p>
          </TicketCard>
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-2">Job earnings history</p>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {requests.filter((r) => r.status === "completed").length === 0 && (
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-6">No completed jobs yet</p>
              )}
              {requests.filter((r) => r.status === "completed").map((r) => (
                <div key={r.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm">{r.customer}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{r.area}</p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-mono-tk text-sm text-emerald-600">+{fmt(r.earned - SERVICE_FEE)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <AdminAccountDisplay account={adminAccount} />
        </div>
      )}

      {completeModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full space-y-3">
            <p className="font-semibold">Mark job complete</p>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 block">Amount charged to customer</label>
            <input value={earned} onChange={(e) => setEarned(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="e.g. 1500"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Rs. {SERVICE_FEE} platform fee will be deducted from this automatically</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setCompleteModal(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full disabled={!earned} onClick={completeJob}>Confirm</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <BottomNav tabs={tabs} active={tab} setActive={setTab} />
    </div>
  );
}

function ServiceProviderProfileForm({ initial, onSave, onSubmit }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name || "");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initial?.category || SERVICE_CATEGORIES[0].id);
  const [area, setArea] = useState(initial?.area || "");
  const [experience, setExperience] = useState(initial?.experience || "");
  const [charges, setCharges] = useState(initial?.charges || "");
  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState(initial?.availability || "Available today");
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState(initial?.photo || null);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  const save = () => {
    onSave({ name, category, area, experience, charges, availability, photo });
    setSaved(true); setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl py-6 cursor-pointer text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500">
        {photo ? <img src={photo} alt="" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full object-cover" /> : <User size={22} />}
        <span className="text-xs">{photo ? "Change photo" : "Add profile photo"}</span>
        <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if (f) setPhoto(URL.createObjectURL(f)); }} />
      </label>
      <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Business / your name"
        className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
      <select value={category} onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
        {SERVICE_CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
      </select>
      <input value={area} onChange={(e) => setArea(e.target.value)} placeholder="Areas you cover (e.g. Gulberg, DHA)"
        className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
      <input value={experience} onChange={(e) => setExperience(e.target.value)} placeholder="Experience (e.g. 8 years)"
        className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
      <input value={charges} onChange={(e) => setCharges(e.target.value)} placeholder="Charges (e.g. Rs. 500 visit + parts)"
        className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
      <select value={availability} onChange={(e) => setAvailability(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
        {["Available today", "Available tomorrow", "Available this week", "Available in 2 days"].map((a) => <option key={a}>{a}</option>)}
      </select>
      <PrimaryButton full onClick={save} disabled={!name || !area}>Save Profile</PrimaryButton>
      {saved && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 text-center">Saved ✓</p>}
      {initial && initial.status !== "pending" && initial.status !== "live" && (
        <GhostButton full onClick={onSubmit}>Submit for Admin Approval</GhostButton>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperOnboarding({ onSave, t }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [address, setAddress] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("General Store");
  const [pinSet, setPinSet] = useState(false);
  const [phones, setPhones] = useState([""]);
  const [photos, setPhotos] = useState([]); // {url, name}

  const addPhone = () => setPhones((p) => [...p, ""]);
  const updatePhone = (i, v) => setPhones((p) => p.map((x, idx) => idx === i ? v : x));
  const removePhone = (i) => setPhones((p) => p.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));

  const addPhoto = (file) => {
    if (!file || photos.length >= 3) return;
    setPhotos((p) => [...p, { url: URL.createObjectURL(file), name: file.name }]);
  };
  const removePhoto = (i) => setPhotos((p) => p.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));

  const categories = ["General Store", "Grocery", "Pharmacy", "Bakery", "Meat & Poultry", "Vegetables & Fruits", "Electronics", "Mobile & Accessories", "Clothing & Fashion", "Stationery & Books", "Hardware", "Cosmetics & Beauty", "Restaurant / Food", "Other"];

  const canSave = name && address && pinSet && phones.some((p) => p.trim());

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen py-8 px-6">
      <div className="mb-6 text-center">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-orange-500 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4"><Store size={26} className="text-white" /></div>
        <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mb-1">Welcome! Let's set up your shop</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">One-time setup — riders will see this to find & contact you</p>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Shop name</label>
          <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Rahim Store"
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Shop address</label>
          <input value={address} onChange={(e) => setAddress(e.target.value)} placeholder="Full address for pickup"
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Pin location</label>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setPinSet(true)}
            className={`w-full flex items-center gap-2 border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm transition ${pinSet ? "border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700" : "border-dashed border-stone-300 dark:border-stone-600 text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500"}`}>
            <MapPin size={16} /> {pinSet ? "Pin location set ✓ — tap to adjust" : "Tap to drop a pin on the map"}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Contact number(s)</label>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {phones.map((p, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">
                <input value={p} onChange={(e) => updatePhone(i, e.target.value)} placeholder="03XX XXXXXXX"
                  className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
                {phones.length > 1 && <button onClick={() => removePhone(i)} className="text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500"><X size={16} /></button>}
              </div>
            ))}
            <button onClick={addPhone} className="text-xs text-orange-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Plus size={13} /> Add another number</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Category</label>
          <select value={category} onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
            {categories.map((c) => <option key={c}>{c}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Shop front photos <span className="text-stone-400 font-normal">(up to 3)</span></label>
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
            {photos.map((p, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="relative w-16 h-16">
                <img src={p.url} alt="" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl object-cover border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700" />
                <button onClick={() => removePhoto(i)} className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 w-5 h-5 bg-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"><X size={11} /></button>
              </div>
            ))}
            {photos.length < 3 && (
              <label className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500 transition">
                <Camera size={18} />
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { addPhoto(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
              </label>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <PrimaryButton full className="mt-6" disabled={!canSave} onClick={() => onSave({ name, address, category, phones: phones.filter(p => p.trim()), photos, pinSet })}>
        Save & Continue <ChevronRight size={16} />
      </PrimaryButton>
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperNewOrders({ onPlace }) {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(1);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([{ address: "", phone: "", alreadyPaid: false, slipPhoto: null }]);
  const [scheduleOn, setScheduleOn] = useState(false);
  const [schedDate, setSchedDate] = useState("");
  const [schedTime, setSchedTime] = useState("");

  const setCountAndResize = (n) => {
    setCount(n);
    setRows((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev];
      while (next.length < n) next.push({ address: "", phone: "", alreadyPaid: false, slipPhoto: null });
      return next.slice(0, n);
    });
  };
  const updateRow = (i, field, value) => setRows((prev) => prev.map((r, idx) => idx === i ? { ...r, [field]: value } : r));

  const canPlace = rows.slice(0, count).every((r) => r.address.trim() && r.phone.trim());

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Zap size={18} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" />
        <p className="text-sm text-orange-800">Pickup & drop only — 40% cheaper than regular delivery</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-2 block">How many orders right now?</label>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((n) => (
            <button key={n} onClick={() => setCountAndResize(n)}
              className={`py-3.5 rounded-xl border font-semibold transition ${count === n ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"}`}>
              {n}{n === 4 ? "+" : ""}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {rows.slice(0, count).map((r, i) => (
          <TicketCard key={i} className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-orange-500">Order {i + 1}</p>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 block">Delivery address</label>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  {["text", "voice", "photo"].map((m) => (
                    <button key={m} onClick={() => updateRow(i, "addressMode", m)}
                      className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-medium ${((r.addressMode || "text") === m) ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700" : "text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500"}`}>
                      {m === "text" ? "Type" : m === "voice" ? "Voice" : "Photo"}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              {(r.addressMode || "text") === "text" && (
                <input value={r.address} onChange={(e) => updateRow(i, "address", e.target.value)} placeholder="Where should this be delivered?"
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
              )}
              {r.addressMode === "voice" && (
                r.address ? (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
                    <Mic size={14} className="text-orange-500" /> <span className="text-xs flex-1">{r.address}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => updateRow(i, "address", "")}><X size={13} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <button onClick={() => updateRow(i, "address", "Voice note — address recorded")}
                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500">
                    <Mic size={14} /> Tap to record the address
                  </button>
                )
              )}
              {r.addressMode === "photo" && (
                r.addressPhoto ? (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
                    <img src={r.addressPhoto} className="w-9 h-9 rounded object-cover" alt="" />
                    <span className="text-xs flex-1">Address photo attached</span>
                    <button onClick={() => { updateRow(i, "addressPhoto", null); updateRow(i, "address", ""); }}><X size={13} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500 cursor-pointer">
                    <Camera size={14} /> Photograph the written address
                    <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => {
                      const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                      if (f) { updateRow(i, "addressPhoto", URL.createObjectURL(f)); updateRow(i, "address", "(see attached photo)"); }
                      e.target.value = "";
                    }} />
                  </label>
                )
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Customer phone number</label>
              <input value={r.phone} onChange={(e) => updateRow(i, "phone", e.target.value)} placeholder="03XX XXXXXXX"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Bill / slip photo <span className="text-stone-400 font-normal">(optional)</span></label>
              {r.slipPhoto ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-lg p-2">
                  <img src={r.slipPhoto.url} alt="" className="w-9 h-9 rounded object-cover" />
                  <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 flex-1 truncate">{r.slipPhoto.name}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => updateRow(i, "slipPhoto", null)}><X size={14} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500">
                  <Camera size={14} /> Attach your handwritten bill / slip
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => {
                    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                    if (f) updateRow(i, "slipPhoto", { url: URL.createObjectURL(f), name: f.name });
                    e.target.value = "";
                  }} />
                </label>
              )}
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={r.alreadyPaid} onChange={(e) => updateRow(i, "alreadyPaid", e.target.checked)} className="accent-orange-500" />
              <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Customer already paid me directly (rider only collects delivery charge)</span>
            </label>
          </TicketCard>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={scheduleOn} onChange={(e) => setScheduleOn(e.target.checked)} className="accent-orange-500" />
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">Schedule for later instead of now</span>
        </label>
        {scheduleOn && (
          <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
            <input type="date" value={schedDate} onChange={(e) => setSchedDate(e.target.value)}
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input type="time" value={schedTime} onChange={(e) => setSchedTime(e.target.value)}
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <PrimaryButton full disabled={!canPlace} onClick={() => onPlace(rows.slice(0, count), scheduleOn ? `${schedDate} ${schedTime}`.trim() : null)}>
        {count === 1 ? "Place Order" : `Place ${count} Orders`} <ChevronRight size={16} />
      </PrimaryButton>
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperStaffPanel() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState([{ name: "Imran (Cashier)", phone: "0300 4455667" }]);
  const [name, setName] = useState(""); const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const add = () => { if (!name) return; setStaff((s) => [...s, { name, phone }]); setName(""); setPhone(""); };
  return (
    <div>
      <button onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)} className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
        Manage staff logins <ChevronRight size={15} className={`text-stone-300 transition ${open ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 mt-2 space-y-2">
          {staff.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between border border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-sm">
              <span>{s.name}</span><span className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.phone}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
            <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Staff name" className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="Phone" className="w-28 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <PrimaryButton small onClick={add}>Add</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperSummaryPanel() {
  const [downloaded, setDownloaded] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div>
      <button onClick={() => { setDownloaded(true); setTimeout(() => setDownloaded(false), 2500); }}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
        Download monthly summary <ChevronRight size={15} className="text-stone-300" />
      </button>
      {downloaded && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 text-center mt-2">Summary for this month is ready — check your downloads ✓</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperOrdersTab({ orders, advance, menuOrders, acceptMenuOrder, completeMenuOrder }) {
  const [view, setView] = useState("menu"); // menu | manual
  const newCount = menuOrders.filter((m) => m.status === "new").length;

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 p-1 rounded-xl">
        <button onClick={() => setView("menu")}
          className={`py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition relative ${view === "menu" ? "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-orange-600 shadow-sm" : "text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
          Menu Orders {newCount > 0 && <span className="ml-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full">{newCount}</span>}
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setView("manual")}
          className={`py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition ${view === "manual" ? "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-orange-600 shadow-sm" : "text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
          Manual Orders
        </button>
      </div>

      {view === "menu" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Orders customers placed directly from your store menu — accept, prepare, and mark complete when the rider collects it</p>
          {menuOrders.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No menu orders yet</p>}
          {menuOrders.map((m) => {
            const itemsTotal = m.items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.price * i.qty, 0);
            return (
              <TicketCard key={m.id} className="p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{m.id}</span>
                  <Badge tone={m.status === "new" ? "amber" : m.status === "accepted" ? "orange" : "green"}>
                    {m.status === "new" ? "New — needs accept" : m.status === "accepted" ? "Preparing" : "Completed"}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">{m.customer}</p>
                <div className="space-y-1 mb-3">
                  {m.items.map((it, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="flex justify-between text-xs text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
                      <span>{it.qty} × {it.name}</span>
                      <span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(it.price * it.qty)}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-semibold border-t border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 pt-2 mb-3">
                  <span>Total</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(itemsTotal)}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-3">{timeAgo(m.createdAt)}</p>
                {m.status === "new" && <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => acceptMenuOrder(m.id)}>Accept Order</PrimaryButton>}
                {m.status === "accepted" && (
                  <>
                    <p className="text-xs text-orange-600 mb-2">Pack these items — rider will collect and pay you {fmt(itemsTotal)}</p>
                    <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => completeMenuOrder(m.id)}>Mark Complete (Rider Collected)</PrimaryButton>
                  </>
                )}
                {m.status === "completed" && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-600 text-xs"><CheckCircle2 size={14} /> Sale recorded — {fmt(itemsTotal)}</div>
                )}
              </TicketCard>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {view === "manual" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {orders.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No manual pickup orders yet</p>}
          {orders.map((o) => (
            <TicketCard key={o.id} className="p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <span className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{o.id}</span>
                <Badge tone="orange">{STATUS_LABEL[o.status]}</Badge>
                {o.alreadyPaid && <Badge tone="green">Prepaid by customer</Badge>}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">{o.text}</p>
              {o.dropPhone && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1 font-mono-tk">{o.dropPhone}</p>}
              <div className="mt-3"><StatusTimeline status={o.status} /></div>
              {o.bill.uploaded && (
                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">
                  Rider payment for this order: <span className="font-mono-tk font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{fmt(o.bill.amount)}</span> — {o.bill.amount > 0 ? "Received ✓" : "Nothing due (prepaid)"}
                </div>
              )}
              {o.status !== "delivered" && (
                <button onClick={() => advance(o.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-500 underline mt-3">simulate next status (demo)</button>
              )}
            </TicketCard>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ShopkeeperApp({ t, lang, setLang, onExit, dark, setDark, allOrders, setAllOrders, adminAccount }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("home");
  const orders = allOrders.filter((o) => o.type === "shopkeeper");
  const setOrders = (updater) => {
    setAllOrders((prevAll) => {
      const mine = prevAll.filter((o) => o.type === "shopkeeper");
      const others = prevAll.filter((o) => o.type !== "shopkeeper");
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(mine) : updater;
      return [...next, ...others];
    });
  };
  const [shopProfile, setShopProfile] = useState(null); // null until onboarded
  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState(-500);
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [storeStatus, setStoreStatus] = useState("draft"); // draft | pending | live | rejected
  const [menuOrders, setMenuOrders] = useState([
    { id: "MO-2201", customer: "Hassan Raza", items: [{ name: "Basmati Rice 5kg", qty: 1, price: 1450 }, { name: "Cooking Oil 3L", qty: 2, price: 980 }], status: "new", createdAt: Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 4 },
    { id: "MO-2198", customer: "Ayesha Khan", items: [{ name: "Sugar 2kg", qty: 1, price: 320 }], status: "accepted", createdAt: Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 22 },
  ]);
  const totalMenuSales = menuOrders.filter((m) => m.status === "completed").reduce((s, m) => s + m.items.reduce((si, i) => si + i.price * i.qty, 0), 0);

  const acceptMenuOrder = (id) => setMenuOrders((prev) => prev.map((m) => m.id === id ? { ...m, status: "accepted" } : m));
  const completeMenuOrder = (id) => setMenuOrders((prev) => prev.map((m) => m.id === id ? { ...m, status: "completed" } : m));

  const tabs = [
    { key: "home", label: t.newOrder, icon: Plus },
    { key: "orders", label: t.orders, icon: ListOrdered },
    { key: "store", label: "My Store", icon: Store },
    { key: "dashboard", label: t.dashboard, icon: LayoutGrid },
    { key: "profile", label: t.profile, icon: User },
  ];

  const advance = (id) => {
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => {
      if (o.id !== id) return o;
      const idx = STATUS_STEPS.indexOf(o.status);
      return { ...o, status: STATUS_STEPS[Math.min(idx + 1, STATUS_STEPS.length - 1)] };
    }));
  };

  const notifications = [
    ...menuOrders.filter((m) => m.status === "new").map((m) => ({ text: `New menu order from ${m.customer} — ${m.items.length} item(s)`, time: timeAgo(m.createdAt), tab: "orders" })),
    { text: "Rider picked up your order TK-1035", time: "6m ago", orderId: "TK-1035" },
    { text: "New regular customer: 0333 2211445 (7 orders)", time: "3h ago", tab: "dashboard" },
  ];
  const openNotification = (n) => {
    if (n.orderId) setTab("orders");
    else if (n.tab) setTab(n.tab);
  };

  const placeOrders = (deliveries, scheduledFor) => {
    const created = deliveries.map((d) => ({
      id: "TK-" + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000), type: "shopkeeper", zone: "z1", status: "placed",
      riderId: autoAssignRider("z1"), text: `Pickup from ${shopProfile.name} → deliver to ${d.address}`,
      pickup: { shopName: shopProfile.name, shopAddress: shopProfile.address, shopPhones: shopProfile.phones, shopPhotos: shopProfile.photos, slipPhoto: d.slipPhoto },
      dropAddress: d.address, dropPhone: d.phone, alreadyPaid: d.alreadyPaid,
      images: [], urgent: false, createdAt: Date.now(), scheduled: !!scheduledFor, scheduledFor,
      bill: { amount: 0, uploaded: false }, payment: { method: "cash", status: "pending" }, chat: [],
    }));
    setOrders((prev) => [...created, ...prev]);
    setTab("orders");
  };

  if (!shopProfile) {
    return <ShopkeeperOnboarding onSave={setShopProfile} t={t} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen relative pb-24">
      <MobileTopBar title={shopProfile.name} lang={lang} setLang={setLang} onExit={onExit} subtitle="Shopkeeper Portal" dark={dark} setDark={setDark}
        notifications={notifications} onNotificationClick={openNotification} />
      {tab === "home" && <ShopkeeperNewOrders onPlace={placeOrders} />}
      {tab === "orders" && (
        <ShopkeeperOrdersTab orders={orders} advance={advance} menuOrders={menuOrders} acceptMenuOrder={acceptMenuOrder} completeMenuOrder={completeMenuOrder} />
      )}
      {tab === "dashboard" && (
        <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-4">
          <ShopkeeperWalletCard wallet={wallet} setWallet={setWallet} adminAccount={adminAccount} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <StatCard label="Menu sales (completed)" value={fmt(totalMenuSales)} icon={Store} />
            <StatCard label="Manual pickup orders" value={String(orders.length)} icon={Package} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-2">Regular customers</p>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {["0333 2211445", "0345 9988112"].map((c) => (
                <div key={c} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm">
                  <span className="font-mono-tk text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">{c}</span>
                  <Badge tone="green">7 orders</Badge>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === "store" && (
        <ShopkeeperStore products={products} setProducts={setProducts} status={storeStatus} setStatus={setStoreStatus} />
      )}
      {tab === "profile" && (
        <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-3">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <p className="font-medium">Rahim Store</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">General Store · Gulberg Zone</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">Business hours: 9:00 AM – 11:00 PM</p>
          </div>
          <ShopkeeperStaffPanel />
          <ShopkeeperSummaryPanel />
        </div>
      )}
      <BottomNav tabs={tabs} active={tab} setActive={setTab} />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   RIDER APP
   ============================================================ */
function RiderApp({ t, lang, setLang, onExit, dark, setDark, allOrders, setAllOrders, withdrawals, setWithdrawals, adminAccount, riders, setRiders }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("queue");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("available"); // available | offline | break
  const myId = riders[0]?.id || "r1"; // fixed identity for this demo session — this is "your" rider account
  const me = riders.find((r) => r.id === myId) || RIDERS[0];
  const setMe = (updater) => {
    setRiders((prev) => prev.map((r) => r.id === myId ? (typeof updater === "function" ? updater(r) : { ...r, ...updater }) : r));
  };
  const orders = allOrders.filter((o) => o.riderId === me.id);
  const setOrders = (updater) => {
    setAllOrders((prevAll) => {
      const mine = prevAll.filter((o) => o.riderId === me.id);
      const others = prevAll.filter((o) => o.riderId !== me.id);
      const next = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(mine) : updater;
      return [...next, ...others];
    });
  };
  const [openOrder, setOpenOrder] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const active = orders.filter((o) => o.status !== "delivered");
    if (!openOrder || !active.find((o) => o.id === openOrder)) {
      setOpenOrder(active[0]?.id || null);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [orders.length]);
  const [showInbox, setShowInbox] = useState(false);

  const notifications = [
    { text: "New order TK-1042 assigned to you", time: "18m ago", orderId: "TK-1042" },
    { text: "Order TK-1035 timer — 10 minutes remaining", time: "25m ago", orderId: "TK-1035" },
    { text: "You reached #2 on this week's leaderboard", time: "1d ago", tab: "earnings" },
    { text: "Your shift starts in 30 minutes — Gulberg 2PM-8PM", time: "1d ago", tab: "shifts" },
  ];
  const openNotification = (n) => {
    setShowInbox(false);
    if (n.orderId) { setTab("queue"); setOpenOrder(n.orderId); }
    else if (n.tab) { setTab(n.tab); }
  };

  const tabs = [
    { key: "queue", label: "Queue", icon: ListOrdered },
    { key: "earnings", label: "Earnings", icon: Wallet },
    { key: "shifts", label: t.myShifts, icon: Clock },
    { key: "profile", label: t.profile, icon: User },
  ];

  const statusStyle = { available: "bg-emerald-500", offline: "bg-stone-300", break: "bg-amber-500" };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen relative pb-24">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-[#FAF9F6]/90 backdrop-blur border-b border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display font-bold text-lg">{me.name}</h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">
            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${statusStyle[status]}`} /> {t[status] || status} · {ZONES.find(z => z.id === me.zone)?.name || "—"} Zone
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <IconBtn icon={Bell} onClick={() => setShowInbox((s) => !s)} />
            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 rounded-full text-[9px] text-white flex items-center justify-center">{notifications.length}</span>
            {showInbox && (
              <div className="fixed top-16 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-4 sm:w-80 max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-2 z-50">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 px-3 py-2">Notifications</p>
                {notifications.map((n, i) => (
                  <button key={i} onClick={() => openNotification(n)} className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-xl">
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{n.text}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{n.time}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
