          <IconBtn icon={Globe} onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")} />
          <IconBtn icon={dark ? Sun : Moon} onClick={() => setDark((d) => !d)} />
          <IconBtn icon={LogOut} onClick={onExit} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {tab === "queue" && (
        <RiderQueue t={t} orders={orders} setOrders={setOrders} openOrder={openOrder} setOpenOrder={setOpenOrder} status={status} setStatus={setStatus} me={me} setMe={setMe} />
      )}
      {tab === "earnings" && <RiderEarnings t={t} me={me} withdrawals={withdrawals} setWithdrawals={setWithdrawals} adminAccount={adminAccount} />}
      {tab === "shifts" && <RiderShifts t={t} me={me} />}
      {tab === "profile" && <RiderProfile t={t} me={me} setMe={setMe} />}

      <BottomNav tabs={tabs} active={tab} setActive={setTab} />
    </div>
  );
}

function RiderEmptyQueue() {
  const CYCLE = 8; // seconds
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(CYCLE);
  const [pulse, setPulse] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      setCountdown((c) => {
        if (c <= 1) {
          setPulse(true);
          setTimeout(() => setPulse(false), 600);
          return CYCLE;
        }
        return c - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-16 text-center flex flex-col items-center">
      <div className={`w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 transition-transform ${pulse ? "scale-110" : "scale-100"}`}>
        <Bike size={26} className="text-orange-400" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      </div>
      <p className="text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 font-medium mb-1">No orders in your queue right now</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-4">You're online — new orders in your zone will appear here automatically</p>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5">
        <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 ${pulse ? "animate-ping" : ""}`} />
        Checking for new orders... {countdown}s
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function RiderQueue({ t, orders, setOrders, openOrder, setOpenOrder, status, setStatus, me, setMe, customerProfile }) {
  const [reporting, setReporting] = useState(false);
  const [reportReason, setReportReason] = useState("Bike issue / breakdown");
  const [toast, setToast] = useState("");
  const showToast = (msg) => { setToast(msg); setTimeout(() => setToast(""), 3500); };
  const [billModal, setBillModal] = useState(false);
  const [slipPhotos, setSlipPhotos] = useState([]); // array of {url}
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [completeModal, setCompleteModal] = useState(false);
  const [rejectingProof, setRejectingProof] = useState(false);
  const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState("");
  const [received, setReceived] = useState("");

  if (status !== "available") {
    return (
      <div className="px-5 py-16 text-center">
        <PauseCircle size={40} className="text-stone-300 mx-auto mb-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />
        <p className="text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-4">You're currently {status === "offline" ? "offline" : "on break"}</p>
        <PrimaryButton onClick={() => setStatus("available")}>Go Available <PlayCircle size={16} /></PrimaryButton>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const order = orders.find((o) => o.id === openOrder);
  if (!order) return <RiderEmptyQueue />;

  const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === order.zone);
  const idx = STATUS_STEPS.indexOf(order.status);
  const nextLabel = ["Start Preparing", "Mark Packing", "Mark Out for Delivery", "Mark Arrived", "Complete Order"][idx];

  const simpleAdvance = () => {
    const next = STATUS_STEPS[Math.min(idx + 1, STATUS_STEPS.length - 1)];
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, status: next } : o));
  };

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (idx === 1) {
      if (order.type === "shopkeeper" && order.alreadyPaid) {
        // Customer already paid the shop directly — rider only owes/collects the discounted delivery charge, no item bill needed
        const deliveryCharge = zone.shopkeeperCharge;
        const platformFee = zone.platformFee;
        setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? {
          ...o, status: "packing",
          bill: { amount: 0, deliveryCharge, platformFee, total: deliveryCharge + platformFee, uploaded: true },
        } : o));
        return;
      }
      setBillModal(true); return;
    }
    if (idx === 4) { setCompleteModal(true); setReceived(String(order.bill.total || "")); return; } // arrived -> delivered requires payment reconciliation
    simpleAdvance();
  };

  const submitBill = () => {
    const itemsAmount = Number(amount) || 0;
    const deliveryCharge = order.type === "shopkeeper" ? zone.shopkeeperCharge : zone.charge;
    const platformFee = zone.platformFee;
    const total = itemsAmount + deliveryCharge + platformFee;
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? {
      ...o, status: "packing",
      bill: { amount: itemsAmount, deliveryCharge, platformFee, total, uploaded: true, invoicePhotos: slipPhotos.map((s) => s.url) },
    } : o));
    setBillModal(false); setSlipPhotos([]); setAmount("");
  };

  const completeOrder = () => {
    const billTotal = order.bill.total || 0;
    const paid = Number(received) || 0;
    const diff = paid - billTotal; // positive = overpaid, negative = underpaid
    const platformFee = order.bill.platformFee || 0;
    // Rider owes admin (diff + platform fee) if overpaid; admin owes rider if underpaid (rider keeps full amount, admin tops up)
    const walletChange = diff > 0 ? -(diff + platformFee) : diff < 0 ? Math.abs(diff) : -platformFee;
    setMe((prev) => ({ ...prev, wallet: prev.wallet + walletChange }));
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, status: "delivered", paymentReceived: paid, walletAdjustment: diff, deliveredAt: Date.now() } : o));
    setCompleteModal(false); setReceived("");
  };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      {orders.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
          {orders.map((o) => (
            <button key={o.id} onClick={() => setOpenOrder(o.id)}
              className={`shrink-0 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono-tk border ${openOrder === o.id ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
              {o.id}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <TicketCard className="p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <span className="font-mono-tk text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{order.id}</span>
          {order.urgent && <Badge tone="amber">Urgent</Badge>}
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200 mb-3 whitespace-pre-wrap">{order.text}</p>

        {/* Step icon strip — 6 stages */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-4">
          {[Clock, Package, FileText, Bike, MapPin, CheckCircle2].map((Icon, i) => (
            <React.Fragment key={i}>
              <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${i <= idx ? "bg-orange-500 text-white" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 text-stone-300"}`}>
                <Icon size={11} strokeWidth={2} />
              </div>
              {i < 5 && <div className={`flex-1 h-0.5 ${i < idx ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700"}`} />}
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </div>

        {order.type === "shopkeeper" ? (
          <div className="space-y-2 mb-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
              {order.pickup?.shopPhotos?.[0] ? (
                <img src={order.pickup.shopPhotos[0].url} alt="" className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg object-cover shrink-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-600" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Store size={18} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{order.pickup?.shopName}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 truncate">{order.pickup?.shopAddress}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {order.pickup?.shopPhones?.[0] && (
                <a href={`tel:${order.pickup.shopPhones[0]}`} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg py-2 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
                  <MessageCircle size={13} /> Call Shop
                </a>
              )}
              <a href={`https://www.google.com/maps/search/${encodeURIComponent(order.pickup?.shopAddress || "")}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"
                className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg py-2 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
                <MapPin size={13} /> Directions
              </a>
            </div>
            {idx >= 1 && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
                <MapPin size={13} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" /> Drop: {order.dropAddress}
                <span className="text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-600">·</span>
                <span className="font-mono-tk">{order.dropPhone}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {order.alreadyPaid && (
              <Badge tone="green">Customer already paid the shop — collect delivery charge only</Badge>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : idx >= 1 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-2 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
            <User size={13} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" /> {customerProfile?.name || "Customer"}
            <span className="text-stone-300 dark:text-stone-600">·</span>
            <MapPin size={13} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" /> {order.address || `${zone.name}, ${CITY}`}
          </div>
        )}
        <LiveTimer startedAt={order.createdAt} minutes={zone.timer} />
      </TicketCard>

      {order.bill.uploaded && (
        <TicketCard className="p-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2">Bill Summary</p>
          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Items purchased</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.amount)}</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Delivery charge</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.deliveryCharge ?? zone.charge)}</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Platform fee</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.platformFee ?? zone.platformFee)}</span></div>
            <div className="border-t border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 my-1" />
            <div className="flex justify-between font-semibold"><span>Total (customer pays)</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.total ?? order.bill.amount)}</span></div>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">Wallet adjustment (if any) will be applied automatically at checkout.</p>
        </TicketCard>
      )}

      {order.paymentProof && (
        <TicketCard className="p-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Camera size={14} /> Customer's Payment Screenshot</p>
          <img src={order.paymentProof.url} alt="Payment proof" className="w-full max-h-48 object-cover rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 mb-3" />
          {order.paymentProof.status === "pending" && (
            <>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setRejectingProof(true)}>Reject</GhostButton>
                <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, paymentProof: { ...o.paymentProof, status: "approved" } } : o))}>
                  <Check size={14} /> Approve Payment
                </PrimaryButton>
              </div>
              {rejectingProof && (
                <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                  <input value={rejectReason} onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)} placeholder="Reason for rejecting (e.g. amount doesn't match)"
                    className="w-full border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none focus:border-red-400" />
                  <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => { setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, paymentProof: { ...o.paymentProof, status: "rejected", reason: rejectReason } } : o)); setRejectingProof(false); setRejectReason(""); }} disabled={!rejectReason.trim()}>
                    Confirm Rejection
                  </PrimaryButton>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
          {order.paymentProof.status === "approved" && <Badge tone="green">Payment approved ✓</Badge>}
          {order.paymentProof.status === "rejected" && <Badge tone="red">Rejected — {order.paymentProof.reason}</Badge>}
        </TicketCard>
      )}

      {order.status === "delivered" ? (
        <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-2xl p-4 text-center text-emerald-700 text-sm">
          Order completed ✓
        </div>
      ) : (
        <PrimaryButton full onClick={handleNext}>{nextLabel}</PrimaryButton>
      )}

      {billModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-end justify-center">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <button onClick={() => { setBillModal(false); setSlipPhotos([]); setAmount(""); }}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">
                <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back
              </button>
              <p className="font-display font-bold">Upload Purchase Bill</p>
              <button onClick={() => { setBillModal(false); setSlipPhotos([]); setAmount(""); }}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" /></button>
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-2">Bought from multiple shops? Photograph each invoice separately.</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                {slipPhotos.map((s, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="relative w-16 h-16">
                    <img src={s.url} alt="" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl object-cover border border-emerald-200" />
                    <button onClick={() => setSlipPhotos((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))}
                      className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 w-5 h-5 bg-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"><X size={11} /></button>
                  </div>
                ))}
                <label className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 transition cursor-pointer">
                  <Camera size={18} />
                  <span className="text-[9px]">{slipPhotos.length ? "Add more" : "Camera"}</span>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" className="hidden"
                    onChange={(e) => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if (f) setSlipPhotos((prev) => [...prev, { url: URL.createObjectURL(f) }]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
                </label>
                <label className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700 transition cursor-pointer">
                  <ImageIcon size={18} />
                  <span className="text-[9px]">Gallery</span>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden"
                    onChange={(e) => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if (f) setSlipPhotos((prev) => [...prev, { url: URL.createObjectURL(f) }]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
                </label>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">If Camera doesn't open a picker on your device, use Gallery instead — it works everywhere.</p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Total purchase amount</label>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-2">Add every invoice together — e.g. Rs. 50 + Rs. 50 = Rs. 100</p>
              <input value={amount} onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="e.g. 780"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk text-lg" />
            </div>

            {amount && (
              <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-1 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"><span>Items</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(Number(amount) || 0)}</span></div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"><span>+ Delivery charge</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(zone.charge)}</span></div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"><span>+ Platform fee</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(zone.platformFee)}</span></div>
                <div className="border-t border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30 my-1" />
                <div className="flex justify-between font-semibold text-orange-800"><span>Total customer bill</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt((Number(amount) || 0) + zone.charge + zone.platformFee)}</span></div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => { setBillModal(false); setSlipPhotos([]); setAmount(""); }}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={submitBill} disabled={slipPhotos.length === 0 || !amount}>Save Bill & Continue</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
            {(slipPhotos.length === 0 || !amount) && (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-600 text-center">
                {slipPhotos.length === 0 && !amount ? "Add at least one invoice photo and the amount to continue" :
                 slipPhotos.length === 0 ? "Add at least one invoice photo to continue" : "Enter the total amount to continue"}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {completeModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-end justify-center">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <button onClick={() => setCompleteModal(false)} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-700">
                <ChevronLeft size={16} /> Back
              </button>
              <p className="font-display font-bold">Complete Order</p>
              <button onClick={() => setCompleteModal(false)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" /></button>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"><span>Bill amount (what customer owes)</span><span className="font-mono-tk">{fmt(order.bill.total || 0)}</span></div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-1.5 block">Amount customer actually paid you</label>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-2">If they paid more or less than the bill, enter the exact amount — the difference settles with Admin automatically.</p>
              <input value={received} onChange={(e) => setReceived(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="e.g. 850"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk text-lg" />
            </div>

            {received && (() => {
              const diff = (Number(received) || 0) - (order.bill.total || 0);
              if (diff === 0) return <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600">Exact amount — no wallet adjustment needed.</p>;
              if (diff > 0) return <p className="text-xs text-amber-600">Customer overpaid by {fmt(diff)} — this + platform fee will be deducted from your wallet (owed to Admin).</p>;
              return <p className="text-xs text-blue-600">Customer underpaid by {fmt(Math.abs(diff))} — Admin will top up your wallet for the shortfall.</p>;
            })()}

            <PrimaryButton full onClick={completeOrder} disabled={!received}>Confirm & Complete Order</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <button onClick={() => setReporting((r) => !r)} className="w-full text-center text-xs text-red-500 underline">Report an issue / request reassignment</button>
      {reporting && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-100 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2">
          <select value={reportReason} onChange={(e) => setReportReason(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none bg-white dark:bg-stone-800">
            <option>Bike issue / breakdown</option>
            <option>Item not available anywhere nearby</option>
            <option>Customer unreachable</option>
            <option>Other</option>
          </select>
          <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => { setReporting(false); showToast(`"${reportReason}" sent to Admin — order will be reassigned`); }}>
            Send to Admin
          </PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}

      <ChatBox chat={order.chat} self="rider" title="Chat with customer"
        deliveredAt={order.status === "delivered" ? (order.deliveredAt || order.createdAt) : null}
        onSend={(m) => setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, chat: [...o.chat, m] } : o))} />

      <button onClick={() => setStatus("break")} className="w-full text-center text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 underline">Take a break</button>
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-stone-800 text-white text-sm px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full shadow-lg z-50 max-w-[85%] text-center">{toast}</div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function RiderEarnings({ t, me, withdrawals, setWithdrawals, adminAccount }) {
  const [showWithdraw, setShowWithdraw] = useState(false);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [showPay, setShowPay] = useState(false);
  const [paySubmitted, setPaySubmitted] = useState(false);
  const myWithdrawals = withdrawals.filter((w) => w.riderId === me.id);
  const pending = myWithdrawals.find((w) => w.status === "pending");
  const awaitingConfirm = myWithdrawals.find((w) => w.status === "awaiting_confirmation");
  const owesCompany = me.wallet < 0;

  const submit = () => {
    setWithdrawals((prev) => [...prev, { id: "w" + Date.now(), riderId: me.id, rider: me.name, amount: Number(amount), status: "pending" }]);
    setShowWithdraw(false);
    setAmount("");
  };
  const confirmReceived = (id) => setWithdrawals((prev) => prev.map((w) => w.id === id ? { ...w, status: "paid" } : w));
  const rejectPayout = (id, reason) => setWithdrawals((prev) => prev.map((w) => w.id === id ? { ...w, status: "rejected", rejectReason: reason } : w));

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-5">
      <TicketCard className="p-6 text-center">
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1">{t.walletBalance}</p>
        <p className={`font-display text-3xl font-bold font-mono-tk ${owesCompany ? "text-red-500" : ""}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(me.wallet))}</p>
        {owesCompany && <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">You owe this to the company</p>}

        {owesCompany ? (
          <>
            {!paySubmitted && (
              <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowPay((s) => !s)} className="mt-4 mx-auto">Pay Admin</PrimaryButton>
            )}
            {showPay && !paySubmitted && (
              <div className="mt-4 text-left space-y-2">
                <div className="bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3">
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-2">Transfer {fmt(Math.abs(me.wallet))} to Taskly's account:</p>
                  <AdminAccountDisplay account={adminAccount} />
                </div>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 cursor-pointer hover:border-orange-300">
                  <Camera size={14} /> Upload payment screenshot
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) { setPaySubmitted(true); setShowPay(false); } }} />
                </label>
              </div>
            )}
            {paySubmitted && (
              <div className="mt-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 rounded-lg p-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 justify-center">
                <Clock size={14} className="text-amber-600" />
                <span className="text-xs text-amber-700">Screenshot sent — Admin will verify and clear your balance</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {!pending && !awaitingConfirm && (
              <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowWithdraw((s) => !s)} className="mt-4 mx-auto">{t.withdrawRequest}</PrimaryButton>
            )}
            {showWithdraw && !pending && !awaitingConfirm && (
              <div className="mt-4 text-left space-y-2">
                <input value={amount} onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder={`Up to ${fmt(me.wallet)}`}
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
                <PrimaryButton small full onClick={submit} disabled={!amount || Number(amount) > me.wallet}>Send request to Admin</PrimaryButton>
              </div>
            )}
            {pending && (
              <div className="mt-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 rounded-lg p-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 justify-center">
                <Clock size={14} className="text-amber-600" />
                <span className="text-xs text-amber-700">Withdraw request of {fmt(pending.amount)} sent — pending Admin approval</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {awaitingConfirm && (
              <div className="mt-3 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-100 dark:border-orange-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 text-left space-y-2">
                <p className="text-xs text-orange-700 font-medium">Admin sent {fmt(awaitingConfirm.amount)} — confirm you received it</p>
                {awaitingConfirm.screenshotUrl && <img src={awaitingConfirm.screenshotUrl} alt="" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-orange-200" />}
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <GhostButton small full onClick={() => { const r = prompt("Reason for rejecting?"); if (r) rejectPayout(awaitingConfirm.id, r); }}>Reject</GhostButton>
                  <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => confirmReceived(awaitingConfirm.id)}>Confirm Received</PrimaryButton>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </TicketCard>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        <StatCard label="Today" value={fmt(650)} icon={TrendingUp} compact />
        <StatCard label="This week" value={fmt(4120)} icon={TrendingUp} compact />
        <StatCard label="Completed" value={me.completed} icon={CheckCircle2} compact />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Daily target</p>
          <span className="text-xs text-orange-600 font-medium">6 / 10 orders</span>
        </div>
        <div className="w-full h-2 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div className="h-full bg-orange-500 rounded-full" style={{ width: "60%" }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Award size={15} className="text-orange-500" /> Leaderboard rank</p>
        <p className="text-2xl font-display font-bold text-orange-600">#2 <span className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-normal">this week</span></p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2">Today's cash purchases</p>
        <p className="text-lg font-mono-tk text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{fmt(2340)}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">across 4 orders</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function RiderShifts({ t, me }) {
  const myZoneName = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === me.zone)?.name || "Your Zone";
  const [shifts, setShifts] = useState([
    { zone: myZoneName, time: "8:00 AM – 2:00 PM", taken: true },
    { zone: myZoneName, time: "2:00 PM – 8:00 PM", taken: false },
    { zone: "DHA", time: "10:00 AM – 6:00 PM", taken: false },
  ]);
  const pickUp = (i) => setShifts((prev) => prev.map((s, idx) => idx === i ? { ...s, taken: true } : s));
  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1">Shifts created by Admin for your area — pick one that suits your schedule</p>
      {shifts.map((s, i) => (
        <div key={i} className={`flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl p-4 border ${s.taken ? "bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 border-orange-200 dark:border-orange-500/30" : "bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700"}`}>
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.zone}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.time}</p>
          </div>
          {s.taken ? <Badge tone="orange">Your shift</Badge> : <GhostButton small onClick={() => pickUp(i)}>Pick up</GhostButton>}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function RiderProfile({ t, me, setMe }) {
  const [openPanel, setOpenPanel] = useState(null); // 'account' | 'training'
  const [account, setAccount] = useState(me.account || { holderName: me.name, bank: "", number: "", iban: "" });
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [copiedField, setCopiedField] = useState(null);
  const [sosConfirm, setSosConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [sosSent, setSosSent] = useState(false);

  const saveAccount = () => {
    setMe((prev) => ({ ...prev, account }));
    setSaved(true); setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2500);
  };
  const copyField = (field, value) => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(value);
    setCopiedField(field); setTimeout(() => setCopiedField(null), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5 space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 font-semibold">{me.name.split(" ").map(w => w[0]).join("").slice(0,2)}</div>
        <div>
          <p className="font-medium">{me.name}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Star size={11} className="fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" /> {me.rating} · {me.completed} deliveries</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-1">Digital ID Badge</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-3">Show this to shopkeepers for verification</p>
        <div className="bg-orange-500 rounded-xl p-4 text-white">
          <p className="text-xs opacity-80">Taskly Authorized Rider</p>
          <p className="font-semibold">{me.name}</p>
          <p className="font-mono-tk text-xs opacity-80 mt-2">ID: TK-RDR-0{me.id.slice(1)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button onClick={() => setOpenPanel(openPanel === "account" ? null : "account")}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
        Account details for online payments <ChevronRight size={15} className={`text-stone-300 transition ${openPanel === "account" ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
      </button>
      {openPanel === "account" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Account holder name</label>
            <input value={account.holderName} onChange={(e) => setAccount((a) => ({ ...a, holderName: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. Asad Ali"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Bank / JazzCash / Easypaisa</label>
            <input value={account.bank} onChange={(e) => setAccount((a) => ({ ...a, bank: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. JazzCash, Meezan Bank"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Account / wallet number</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input value={account.number} onChange={(e) => setAccount((a) => ({ ...a, number: e.target.value }))} placeholder="03XX-XXXXXXX"
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              <button onClick={() => copyField("number", account.number)} className="px-3 rounded-lg border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 shrink-0">
                {copiedField === "number" ? <Check size={14} className="text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy size={14} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">IBAN <span className="font-normal">(for bank transfers, optional)</span></label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input value={account.iban} onChange={(e) => setAccount((a) => ({ ...a, iban: e.target.value }))} placeholder="PK00-BANK-0000000000000000"
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              <button onClick={() => copyField("iban", account.iban)} className="px-3 rounded-lg border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 shrink-0">
                {copiedField === "iban" ? <Check size={14} className="text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy size={14} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton small full onClick={saveAccount}>Save</PrimaryButton>
          {saved && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 text-center">Saved ✓ — customers will see this for online transfers</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      <button onClick={() => setOpenPanel(openPanel === "training" ? null : "training")}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
        Training & onboarding guide <ChevronRight size={15} className={`text-stone-300 transition ${openPanel === "training" ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
      </button>
      {openPanel === "training" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2 text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">
          {["Always confirm total amount before leaving the shop", "Photograph every invoice clearly before uploading", "Never share customer contact details outside the app", "Use the SOS button immediately in any unsafe situation"].map((tip, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex gap-2"><CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" /><span>{tip}</span></div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <button onClick={() => setSosConfirm(true)} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-red-50 text-red-500 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-red-100 transition">
        <ShieldAlert size={16} /> SOS — Emergency Alert
      </button>

      {sosConfirm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-xs w-full text-center">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
              <ShieldAlert size={18} className="text-red-500" />
            </div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-stone-800 dark:text-stone-100 mb-1">Send emergency alert?</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-5">Admin and our helpline will be notified immediately with your current order and location.</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setSosConfirm(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <button onClick={() => { setSosConfirm(false); setSosSent(true); setTimeout(() => setSosSent(false), 4000); }}
                className="w-full px-5 py-3 bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl font-medium transition">
                Send SOS
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {sosSent && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full px-5 py-3 shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium z-40">
          <ShieldAlert size={15} /> SOS sent — Admin is on the way
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN APP (desktop-focused)
   ============================================================ */
function AdminApp({ t, lang, setLang, onExit, dark, setDark, allOrders, setAllOrders, withdrawals, setWithdrawals, adminAccount, setAdminAccount, serviceProviders, setServiceProviders }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("dashboard");
  const orders = allOrders;
  const setOrders = setAllOrders;
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState(RIDERS);
  const [zones, setZones] = useState(ZONES);
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showInbox, setShowInbox] = useState(false);

  const notifications = [
    { text: "Order TK-1042 is running late in Gulberg zone", time: "5m ago", tab: "orders" },
    { text: "New dispute filed on order TK-1039", time: "1h ago", tab: "disputes" },
    { text: "Rider Bilal Khan requested reassignment", time: "2h ago", tab: "orders" },
    { text: "Withdraw request from Asad Ali — Rs. 3,450", time: "3h ago", tab: "riders" },
  ];
  const openNotification = (n) => { setShowInbox(false); setTab(n.tab); };

  const NotifBell = () => (
    <div className="relative">
      <IconBtn icon={Bell} onClick={() => setShowInbox((s) => !s)} />
      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-orange-500 rounded-full text-[9px] text-white flex items-center justify-center">{notifications.length}</span>
      {showInbox && (
        <div className="fixed top-16 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-4 sm:w-80 max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-2 z-50">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 px-3 py-2">Notifications</p>
          {notifications.map((n, i) => (
            <button key={i} onClick={() => openNotification(n)} className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-xl">
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{n.text}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{n.time}</p>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  const nav = [
    { key: "dashboard", label: t.dashboard, icon: LayoutGrid },
    { key: "ordermgmt", label: "Order Management", icon: ListOrdered },
    { key: "wallets", label: "Wallets", icon: Wallet },
    { key: "riders", label: "Rider Management", icon: Bike },
    { key: "customermgmt", label: "Customer Management", icon: Users },
    { key: "shopkeepermgmt", label: "Shopkeeper Management", icon: Store },
    { key: "zones", label: t.zones, icon: MapPin },
    { key: "providers", label: "Service Providers", icon: Settings },
    { key: "disputes", label: t.disputes, icon: AlertTriangle },
    { key: "promos", label: t.promoCodes, icon: Ticket },
    { key: "settings", label: "Settings", icon: Settings },
  ];
  const mobileNav = [nav[0], nav[1], nav[3], nav[4]]; // primary 4 for bottom bar

  const NavLinks = ({ onNavigate }) => (
    <>
      {nav.map((n) => (
        <button key={n.key} onClick={() => { setTab(n.key); onNavigate && onNavigate(); }}
          className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm transition ${tab === n.key ? "bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700 font-medium" : "text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700"}`}>
          <n.icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} /> {n.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col md:flex-row">
      {/* Mobile top bar */}
      <div className="md:hidden sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border-b border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
        <button onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(true)} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center">
          <LayoutGrid size={17} className="text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" />
        </button>
        <span className="font-display font-bold text-sm">{t.appName} <span className="text-orange-500 font-normal">Admin</span></span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <NotifBell />
          <IconBtn icon={Globe} onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")} />
          <IconBtn icon={dark ? Sun : Moon} onClick={() => setDark((d) => !d)} />
          <IconBtn icon={LogOut} onClick={onExit} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile drawer */}
      {drawerOpen && (
        <div className="md:hidden fixed inset-0 z-40 flex">
          <div className="w-64 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 h-full flex flex-col shadow-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
              <span className="font-display font-bold text-sm">Menu</span>
              <button onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" /></button>
            </div>
            <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto">
              <NavLinks onNavigate={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} />
            </nav>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 bg-black/30" onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Desktop sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden md:flex w-60 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border-r border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 flex-col shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-5 border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-orange-500 flex items-center justify-center"><Package size={16} className="text-white" /></div>
          <span className="font-display font-bold">{t.appName} <span className="text-orange-500 font-normal text-sm">Admin</span></span>
        </div>
        <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-1">
          <NavLinks />
        </nav>
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 space-y-1">
          <button onClick={() => setLang(lang === "en" ? "ur" : "en")} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700">
            <Globe size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} /> {lang === "en" ? "اردو" : "English"}
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => setDark((d) => !d)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700">
            {dark ? <Sun size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} /> : <Moon size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />} {dark ? "Light mode" : "Dark mode"}
          </button>
          <button onClick={onExit} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700">
            <LogOut size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} /> {t.logout}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto md:max-h-screen pb-20 md:pb-0">
        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center justify-end gap-2 px-8 pt-5">
          <NotifBell />
        </div>
        {tab === "dashboard" && <AdminDashboard t={t} orders={orders} />}
        {tab === "ordermgmt" && <AdminOrderManagement orders={orders} riders={riders} setOrders={setOrders} />}
        {tab === "wallets" && <AdminWallets t={t} riders={riders} setRiders={setRiders} withdrawals={withdrawals} setWithdrawals={setWithdrawals} adminAccount={adminAccount} />}
        {tab === "riders" && <AdminRiders t={t} riders={riders} setRiders={setRiders} orders={orders} />}
        {tab === "customermgmt" && <AdminCustomerManagement orders={orders} />}
        {tab === "shopkeepermgmt" && <AdminShopkeeperManagement orders={orders} />}
        {tab === "zones" && <AdminZones t={t} zones={zones} setZones={setZones} />}
        {tab === "providers" && <AdminServiceProviders providers={serviceProviders} setProviders={setServiceProviders} />}
        {tab === "disputes" && <AdminDisputes t={t} />}
        {tab === "promos" && <AdminPromos t={t} />}
        {tab === "settings" && <AdminSettings t={t} adminAccount={adminAccount} setAdminAccount={setAdminAccount} />}
      </main>

      {/* Mobile bottom nav — quick access to primary sections */}
      <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-20 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border-t border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 px-1 py-1.5 flex justify-around">
        {mobileNav.map((n) => (
          <button key={n.key} onClick={() => setTab(n.key)}
            className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition ${tab === n.key ? "text-orange-600" : "text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500"}`}>
            <n.icon size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            <span className="text-[9px] font-medium">{n.label}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
        <button onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(true)} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">
          <Settings size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-medium">More</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({ label, value, icon: Icon, compact, trend }) {
  return (
    <div className={`bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl ${compact ? "p-3" : "p-4"}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center"><Icon size={15} className="text-orange-500" strokeWidth={1.8} /></div>
        {trend && <span className={`text-xs flex items-center gap-0.5 ${trend > 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-500"}`}>
          {trend > 0 ? <ArrowUpRight size={12} /> : <ArrowDownRight size={12} />}{Math.abs(trend)}%
        </span>}
      </div>
      <p className={`font-display font-bold ${compact ? "text-lg" : "text-2xl"} font-mono-tk`}>{value}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminOrderManagement({ orders, riders, setOrders }) {
  const [section, setSection] = useState("active"); // active | pending | completed | cancelled | all
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [zoneFilter, setZoneFilter] = useState("all");
  const [riderFilter, setRiderFilter] = useState("all");
  const [reassigning, setReassigning] = useState(null);

  const active = orders.filter((o) => !["delivered"].includes(o.status) && !o.disputeStatus && !o.rejected);
  const pending = orders.filter((o) => o.status === "placed");
  const completed = orders.filter((o) => o.status === "delivered");
  const cancelled = orders.filter((o) => o.disputeStatus === "open" || o.rejected);
  const cancelRequests = orders.filter((o) => o.cancelRequest?.status === "pending");

  const decideCancelRequest = (orderId, approve, note) => {
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? {
      ...o,
      cancelRequest: { ...o.cancelRequest, status: approve ? "approved" : "rejected", adminNote: note },
      rejected: approve ? true : o.rejected,
    } : o));
  };

  const sections = [
    { key: "active", label: "Active", list: active, tone: "orange" },
    { key: "pending", label: "Pending", list: pending, tone: "amber" },
    { key: "completed", label: "Completed", list: completed, tone: "green" },
    { key: "cancelled", label: "Cancelled/Disputed", list: cancelled, tone: "red" },
    { key: "cancelreq", label: `Cancel Requests${cancelRequests.length ? ` (${cancelRequests.length})` : ""}`, list: cancelRequests, tone: "amber" },
    { key: "all", label: "All Orders", list: orders, tone: "default" },
  ];
  const current = sections.find((s) => s.key === section);

  const filtered = current.list
    .filter((o) => !search.trim() || o.id.toLowerCase().includes(search.trim().toLowerCase()))
    .filter((o) => zoneFilter === "all" || o.zone === zoneFilter)
    .filter((o) => riderFilter === "all" || o.riderId === riderFilter);

  const reassign = (orderId, riderId) => {
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, riderId } : o));
    setReassigning(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Order Management</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Every order on the platform, centrally — search, filter, and manage from one place</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3">
        {sections.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.key} onClick={() => setSection(s.key)}
            className={`text-left bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border rounded-2xl p-4 transition ${section === s.key ? "border-orange-400 ring-2 ring-orange-100 dark:ring-orange-500/20" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700"}`}>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-2xl">{s.list.length}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">{s.label}</p>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
          <Search size={14} className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500" />
          <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search order ID..." className="text-sm outline-none w-36 bg-transparent min-w-0" />
        </div>
        <select value={zoneFilter} onChange={(e) => setZoneFilter(e.target.value)} className="text-sm border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
          <option value="all">All zones</option>
          {ZONES.map((z) => <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>{z.name}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select value={riderFilter} onChange={(e) => setRiderFilter(e.target.value)} className="text-sm border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
          <option value="all">All riders</option>
          {riders.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>

      {section === "cancelreq" ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No pending cancellation requests</p>}
          {filtered.map((o) => {
            const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === o.zone);
            return (
              <div key={o.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{o.id}</span>
                  <Badge tone="amber">{zone?.name}</Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mb-3">"{o.cancelRequest.reason}"</p>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <GhostButton small full onClick={() => decideCancelRequest(o.id, false, "Rider already purchasing — cannot cancel now")}>Reject</GhostButton>
                  <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => decideCancelRequest(o.id, true)}>Approve Cancellation</PrimaryButton>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-xs">
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Order</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Zone</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Type</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Rider</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Total</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Status</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Action</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {filtered.length === 0 && (
              <tr><td colSpan={7} className="text-center text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 py-8">No orders match</td></tr>
            )}
            {filtered.map((o) => {
              const zone = ZONES.find((z) => z.id === o.zone);
              const rider = riders.find((r) => r.id === o.riderId);
              const zoneRiders = riders.filter((r) => r.zone === o.zone);
              return (
                <tr key={o.id} className="border-b border-stone-50 dark:border-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-stone-700">
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk text-xs">{o.id}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{zone?.name || "—"}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 capitalize">{o.type}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{rider?.name || "—"}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk">{fmt(o.bill?.total ?? o.bill?.amount ?? 0)}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3"><Badge tone={o.disputeStatus ? "red" : o.status === "delivered" ? "green" : "orange"}>{o.disputeStatus ? "Disputed" : STATUS_LABEL[o.status]}</Badge></td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 relative">
                    <button onClick={() => setReassigning(reassigning === o.id ? null : o.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Reassign</button>
                    {reassigning === o.id && (
                      <div className="absolute z-10 top-8 left-4 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl shadow-lg p-2 w-44">
                        {zoneRiders.map((r) => (
                          <button key={r.id} onClick={() => reassign(o.id, r.id)}
                            className="w-full text-left text-xs px-3 py-2 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-500/10 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
                            {r.name} <Badge tone={r.status === "available" ? "green" : "default"}>{r.status}</Badge>
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminDashboard({ t, orders }) {
  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">{t.dashboard}</h2>
        <Badge tone="green">● Live</Badge>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
        <StatCard label="Active orders" value="18" icon={Package} trend={12} />
        <StatCard label="Online riders" value="9 / 14" icon={Bike} />
        <StatCard label="Today's revenue" value={fmt(64200)} icon={Wallet} trend={8} />
        <StatCard label="Avg. rating" value="4.7" icon={Star} />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="col-span-2 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-4">Orders — last 7 days</p>
          <div className="flex items-end gap-3 h-40">
            {[32, 45, 38, 60, 52, 71, 64].map((v, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                <div className="w-full bg-orange-100 rounded-t-lg relative" style={{ height: `${v}%` }}>
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-orange-500 rounded-t-lg opacity-80" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{["M","T","W","T","F","S","S"][i]}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-4">Zone profitability</p>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {ZONES.map((z) => (
              <div key={z.id}>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1"><span>{z.name}</span><span className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{fmt(z.charge * 40)}</span></div>
                <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><div className="h-full bg-orange-500" style={{ width: `${z.charge / 1.5}%` }} /></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-3">Trending items this week</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
          {["Grocery bundles", "Medicine", "Vegetables", "Bakery items", "Meat & Poultry"].map((i) => <Badge key={i} tone="orange">{i}</Badge>)}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminAnnouncements({ defaultType }) {
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [selectedZones, setSelectedZones] = useState([]);
  const [userType, setUserType] = useState(defaultType); // 'customer' | 'rider' | 'both'
  const [specificTarget, setSpecificTarget] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [sentLog, setSentLog] = useState([]);

  const toggleZone = (id) => setSelectedZones((prev) => prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((z) => z !== id) : [...prev, id]);

  const send = () => {
    if (!message.trim()) return;
    const target = specificTarget.trim()
      ? `${specificTarget} only`
      : `${userType === "both" ? "Customers + Riders" : userType === "customer" ? "Customers" : "Riders"} in ${selectedZones.length ? selectedZones.map(id => ZONES.find(z=>z.id===id)?.name).join(", ") : "all zones"}`;
    setSentLog((prev) => [{ id: Date.now(), target, message, time: "just now" }, ...prev]);
    setMessage(""); setSpecificTarget(""); setSelectedZones([]); setShowForm(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{defaultType === "rider" ? "Rider" : "Customer"} Announcements</p>
        <button onClick={() => setShowForm((s) => !s)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">{showForm ? "Cancel" : "New announcement"}</button>
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-3">Target specific zones and user types — never a blanket broadcast to everyone</p>

      {showForm && (
        <div className="space-y-3 mb-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1.5 block">Send to</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {["customer", "rider", "both"].map((ut) => (
                <button key={ut} onClick={() => setUserType(ut)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border capitalize ${userType === ut ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
                  {ut === "both" ? "Both" : ut + "s"}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1.5 block">Zones (leave empty for all zones)</label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {ZONES.map((z) => (
                <button key={z.id} onClick={() => toggleZone(z.id)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] border ${selectedZones.includes(z.id) ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400"}`}>
                  {z.name}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1.5 block">Or target one specific person (overrides zone/type above)</label>
            <input value={specificTarget} onChange={(e) => setSpecificTarget(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Sana Ahmed or Asad Ali"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <textarea value={message} onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)} rows={3} placeholder="Announcement message..."
            className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          <PrimaryButton small full onClick={send} disabled={!message.trim()}><Send size={13} /> Send Announcement</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}

      {sentLog.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
          {sentLog.map((s) => (
            <div key={s.id} className="text-xs bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg p-2.5">
              <p className="text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">{s.message}</p>
              <p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-1">→ {s.target} · {s.time}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminCustomerManagement({ orders }) {
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState([
    { id: "c1", name: "Sana Ahmed", phone: "0300 1234567", balance: -150 },
    { id: "c2", name: "Hassan Raza", phone: "0321 5567890", balance: 320 },
    { id: "c3", name: "Ayesha Khan", phone: "0333 9988776", balance: 0 },
  ]);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(null);
  const settle = (id) => setCustomers((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.id === id ? { ...c, balance: 0 } : c));

  const totalOwedByCustomers = customers.filter(c => c.balance < 0).reduce((s, c) => s + Math.abs(c.balance), 0);
  const totalOwedToCustomers = customers.filter(c => c.balance > 0).reduce((s, c) => s + c.balance, 0);

  // demo: attribute customer-type orders to Sana Ahmed (single seeded customer persona)
  const ordersFor = (name) => name === "Sana Ahmed" ? orders.filter((o) => o.type === "customer" || o.type === "storeOrder") : [];

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Customer Management</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Every customer's wallet, order history, and over/underpayment record</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Customers owe us" value={fmt(totalOwedByCustomers)} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
        <StatCard label="We owe customers" value={fmt(totalOwedToCustomers)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {customers.map((c) => {
          const cOrders = ordersFor(c.name);
          return (
            <div key={c.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{c.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-mono-tk">{c.phone} · {cOrders.length} orders</p>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <Badge tone={c.balance < 0 ? "red" : c.balance > 0 ? "amber" : "green"}>
                    {c.balance < 0 ? `Owes ${fmt(Math.abs(c.balance))}` : c.balance > 0 ? `Refund ${fmt(c.balance)}` : "Settled"}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3">
                <button onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === c.id ? null : c.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">
                  {expanded === c.id ? "Hide" : "View"} order history
                </button>
                {c.balance !== 0 && <button onClick={() => settle(c.id)} className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 underline">Mark settled</button>}
              </div>
              {expanded === c.id && (
                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 space-y-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                  {cOrders.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">No orders yet</p>}
                  {cOrders.map((o) => (
                    <div key={o.id} className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <span className="font-mono-tk text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{o.id}</span>
                      <span className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{fmt(o.bill?.total || 0)}</span>
                      <Badge tone={o.status === "delivered" ? "green" : "orange"}>{STATUS_LABEL[o.status]}</Badge>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <AdminAnnouncements defaultType="customer" />
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminRiders({ t, riders, setRiders, orders }) {
  const setStatus = (id, status) => setRiders((prev) => prev.map((r) => r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState(""); const [phone, setPhone] = useState(""); const [zone, setZone] = useState(ZONES[0].id);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState(""); const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState("");
  const showToast = (msg) => { setToast(msg); setTimeout(() => setToast(""), 3000); };

  const addRider = () => {
    if (!name || !phone || !username) return;
    setRiders((prev) => [...prev, { id: "r" + (prev.length + 1), name, zone, status: "offline", rating: 0, completed: 0, wallet: 0, late: 0, username, password, account: { holderName: name, bank: "", number: "", iban: "" } }]);
    setName(""); setPhone(""); setUsername(""); setPassword(""); setShowForm(false);
  };

  const activeCount = riders.filter((r) => r.status === "available" || r.status === "busy").length;
  const toReceive = riders.filter((r) => r.wallet < 0).reduce((s, r) => s + Math.abs(r.wallet), 0);
  const toPay = riders.filter((r) => r.wallet > 0).reduce((s, r) => s + r.wallet, 0);
  const net = toReceive - toPay;

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Rider Management</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Create rider profiles, manage credentials, and track each rider's performance & wallet</p>
        </div>
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowForm((s) => !s)}><Plus size={14} /> New Rider</PrimaryButton>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Total Riders" value={String(riders.length)} icon={Bike} />
        <StatCard label="Active Riders" value={String(activeCount)} icon={CheckCircle2} />
        <StatCard label="Amount to Receive" value={fmt(toReceive)} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
        <StatCard label="Amount to Pay" value={fmt(toPay)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
      </div>
      <div className={`rounded-2xl p-4 border ${net >= 0 ? "bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-500/30" : "bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30"}`}>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1">Net position across all riders</p>
        <p className={`font-display font-bold text-2xl ${net >= 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-500"}`}>
          {net >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}{fmt(Math.abs(net))} <span className="text-sm font-normal">— company is {net >= 0 ? "Plus" : "Minus"}</span>
        </p>
      </div>
      {showForm && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 uppercase tracking-wide">Profile</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Full name"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="03XX XXXXXXX"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <select value={zone} onChange={(e) => setZone(e.target.value)} className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none bg-white dark:bg-stone-800">
              {ZONES.map((z) => <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>{z.name}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 uppercase tracking-wide pt-1">Login credentials</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <input value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} placeholder="Username"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <input value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Temporary password" type="text"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton small onClick={addRider}>Create Rider Account</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {riders.map((r) => {
          const riderOrders = orders.filter((o) => o.riderId === r.id);
          return (
          <div key={r.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 text-xs font-semibold">
                  {r.name.split(" ").map(w => w[0]).join("")}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{r.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{ZONES.find(z=>z.id===r.zone)?.name}{r.username && <span className="font-mono-tk"> · @{r.username}</span>}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Badge tone={r.status === "available" ? "green" : r.status === "busy" ? "amber" : "red"}>{r.status}</Badge>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-xs mb-3">
              <div><p className="font-mono-tk font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{r.completed}</p><p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">orders</p></div>
              <div><p className="font-mono-tk font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{r.rating}★</p><p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">rating</p></div>
              <div><p className="font-mono-tk font-semibold text-stone-700 dark:text-stone-200">{r.late}</p><p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">late</p></div>
            </div>
            <div className={`flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-3 ${r.wallet < 0 ? "bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10" : r.wallet > 0 ? "bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-500/10" : "bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10"}`}>
              <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{r.wallet < 0 ? "Owes company" : r.wallet > 0 ? "Company owes" : "Wallet settled"}</span>
              <span className={`font-mono-tk text-sm font-semibold ${r.wallet < 0 ? "text-red-500" : r.wallet > 0 ? "text-amber-600" : "text-emerald-600"}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(r.wallet))}</span>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === r.id ? null : r.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline mb-2">
              {expanded === r.id ? "Hide" : "View"} order history ({riderOrders.length})
            </button>
            {expanded === r.id && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5 mb-3 max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
                {riderOrders.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">No orders yet</p>}
                {riderOrders.map((o) => (
                  <div key={o.id} className="flex justify-between text-xs border-b border-stone-50 dark:border-stone-700 pb-1">
                    <span className="font-mono-tk text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{o.id}</span>
                    <Badge tone={o.status === "delivered" ? "green" : "orange"}>{STATUS_LABEL[o.status]}</Badge>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <GhostButton small full onClick={() => showToast(`⚠️ Warning sent to ${r.name}`)}>Warn</GhostButton>
              {r.status === "suspended" ? (
                <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => setStatus(r.id, "available")}>Reinstate</PrimaryButton>
              ) : (
                <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setStatus(r.id, "suspended")}>Suspend</GhostButton>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        );})}
      </div>

      <AdminAnnouncements defaultType="rider" />
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-stone-800 text-white text-sm px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full shadow-lg z-50">{toast}</div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminZones({ t, zones, setZones }) {
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
