  const [name, setName] = useState(""); const [charge, setCharge] = useState(""); const [timer, setTimer] = useState("");
  const [pausingId, setPausingId] = useState(null);
  const [reasonInput, setReasonInput] = useState("");

  const toggle = (id) => {
    const z = zones.find((zn) => zn.id === id);
    if (z.status === "open") { setPausingId(id); setReasonInput(""); return; }
    setZones((prev) => prev.map((zn) => zn.id === id ? { ...zn, status: "open", reason: "" } : zn));
  };
  const confirmPause = () => {
    setZones((prev) => prev.map((z) => z.id === pausingId ? { ...z, status: "paused", reason: reasonInput || "Temporarily unavailable" } : z));
    setPausingId(null);
  };
  const addZone = () => {
    if (!name || !charge) return;
    setZones((prev) => [...prev, { id: "z" + (prev.length + 1), name, charge: Number(charge), platformFee: 15, timer: Number(timer) || 40, shopkeeperCharge: Math.round(Number(charge) * 0.6), status: "open", hours: "8:00 AM – 10:00 PM", reason: "" }]);
    setName(""); setCharge(""); setTimer(""); setShowForm(false);
  };

  const [showBulk, setShowBulk] = useState(false);
  const [bulkPct, setBulkPct] = useState("");
  const applyBulk = (direction) => {
    const pct = Number(bulkPct) || 0;
    if (!pct) return;
    setZones((prev) => prev.map((z) => ({
      ...z,
      charge: Math.round(z.charge * (1 + (direction * pct) / 100)),
      platformFee: Math.round(z.platformFee * (1 + (direction * pct) / 100)),
    })));
    setBulkPct(""); setShowBulk(false);
  };

  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState(null);
  const [shiftsId, setShiftsId] = useState(null);

  const openEdit = (z) => { setEditingId(z.id); setEditForm({ ...z }); };
  const saveEdit = () => {
    setZones((prev) => prev.map((z) => z.id === editingId ? { ...editForm, charge: Number(editForm.charge), platformFee: Number(editForm.platformFee), timer: Number(editForm.timer), shopkeeperCharge: Number(editForm.shopkeeperCharge) } : z));
    setEditingId(null); setEditForm(null);
  };
  const deleteZone = (id) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${zones.find(z=>z.id===id)?.name}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
    setZones((prev) => prev.filter((z) => z.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">{t.zones}</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Areas within {CITY} — each with its own fixed delivery charge, hours & shifts</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <GhostButton small onClick={() => setShowBulk((s) => !s)}><TrendingUp size={14} /> Bulk Update</GhostButton>
          <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowForm((s) => !s)}><Plus size={14} /> Add Area</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>

      {showBulk && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">Apply a percentage change to all areas' delivery charge & platform fee at once</p>
          <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
            <input value={bulkPct} onChange={(e) => setBulkPct(e.target.value)} type="number" placeholder="e.g. 10"
              className="w-32 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <span className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">%</span>
            <GhostButton small onClick={() => applyBulk(1)}><ArrowUpRight size={13} /> Increase all</GhostButton>
            <GhostButton small onClick={() => applyBulk(-1)}><ArrowDownRight size={13} /> Decrease all</GhostButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showForm && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 items-end">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Area name</label>
            <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Iqbal Town"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Delivery charge (Rs.)</label>
            <input value={charge} onChange={(e) => setCharge(e.target.value)} placeholder="110" type="number"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Timer (min)</label>
            <input value={timer} onChange={(e) => setTimer(e.target.value)} placeholder="40" type="number"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton small onClick={addZone}>Create</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}

      {pausingId && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-amber-800">Pause "{zones.find(z=>z.id===pausingId)?.name}" — reason (shown to customers in this area)</p>
          <input value={reasonInput} onChange={(e) => setReasonInput(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Heavy rain, Eid holidays, weekend closure..."
            className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-amber-400" />
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <GhostButton small onClick={() => setPausingId(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
            <PrimaryButton small onClick={confirmPause}>Confirm Pause</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-xs">
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Area</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Delivery charge</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Platform fee</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Timer</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Working Hours</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Shopkeeper rate</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Status</th>
              <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Action</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {zones.map((z) => (
              <tr key={z.id} className="border-b border-stone-50 dark:border-stone-700">
                <td className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">{z.name}</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk">{fmt(z.charge)}</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk">{fmt(z.platformFee)}</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3">{z.timer} min</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3 text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 text-xs">{z.hours}</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk">{fmt(z.shopkeeperCharge)}</td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3">
                  <Badge tone={z.status === "open" ? "green" : "red"}>{z.status}</Badge>
                  {z.status === "paused" && z.reason && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-500 mt-1 max-w-[160px]">{z.reason}</p>}
                </td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => toggle(z.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">{z.status === "open" ? "Pause" : "Resume"}</button>
                    <button onClick={() => openEdit(z)} className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 underline">Edit</button>
                    <button onClick={() => setShiftsId(z.id)} className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 underline">Shifts</button>
                    <button onClick={() => deleteZone(z.id)} className="text-xs text-red-500 underline">Delete</button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {editingId && editForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-md w-full space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <p className="font-display font-bold">Edit {editForm.name}</p>
              <button onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Area name</label>
              <input value={editForm.name} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))}
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Delivery charge</label>
                <input value={editForm.charge} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, charge: e.target.value }))} type="number"
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Platform fee</label>
                <input value={editForm.platformFee} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, platformFee: e.target.value }))} type="number"
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Timer (min)</label>
                <input value={editForm.timer} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, timer: e.target.value }))} type="number"
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Shopkeeper rate</label>
                <input value={editForm.shopkeeperCharge} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, shopkeeperCharge: e.target.value }))} type="number"
                  className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Working hours</label>
              <input value={editForm.hours} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, hours: e.target.value }))} placeholder="8:00 AM – 10:00 PM"
                className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={saveEdit}>Save changes</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {shiftsId && <AreaShiftsModal zoneName={zones.find(z=>z.id===shiftsId)?.name} onClose={() => setShiftsId(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function AreaShiftsModal({ zoneName, onClose }) {
  const [shifts, setShifts] = useState([
    { time: "8:00 AM – 2:00 PM", capacity: 4 },
    { time: "2:00 PM – 8:00 PM", capacity: 4 },
    { time: "8:00 PM – 2:00 AM", capacity: 2 },
  ]);
  const [start, setStart] = useState(""); const [end, setEnd] = useState(""); const [capacity, setCapacity] = useState("2");

  const addShift = () => {
    if (!start || !end) return;
    setShifts((prev) => [...prev, { time: `${start} – ${end}`, capacity: Number(capacity) || 1 }]);
    setStart(""); setEnd(""); setCapacity("2");
  };
  const removeShift = (i) => setShifts((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-md w-full space-y-4 max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="font-display font-bold">Shifts — {zoneName}</p>
          <button onClick={onClose}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Riders in this area will see these shifts on their portal to pick up</p>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          {shifts.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.time}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.capacity} riders capacity</p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => removeShift(i)} className="text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500"><X size={15} /></button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 pt-3 space-y-2">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Add a shift</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <input value={start} onChange={(e) => setStart(e.target.value)} placeholder="8:00 AM"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input value={end} onChange={(e) => setEnd(e.target.value)} placeholder="2:00 PM"
              className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Riders needed:</label>
            <input value={capacity} onChange={(e) => setCapacity(e.target.value)} type="number" className="w-16 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton small full onClick={addShift}>Add Shift</PrimaryButton>
        </div>

        <PrimaryButton full onClick={onClose}>Done</PrimaryButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminServiceProviders({ providers, setProviders }) {
  const setStatus = (id, status) => setProviders((prev) => prev.map((p) => p.id === id ? { ...p, status } : p));
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState(null);

  const openEdit = (p) => { setEditingId(p.id); setEditForm({ ...p }); };
  const saveEdit = () => {
    setProviders((prev) => prev.map((p) => p.id === editingId ? editForm : p));
    setEditingId(null); setEditForm(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Service Providers</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Plumbers, electricians & other home service providers — approve before they go live for customers</p>
      </div>

      {providers.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-center py-8">No service provider signups yet</p>}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {providers.map((p) => (
          <div key={p.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
              {p.photo ? <img src={p.photo} alt="" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full object-cover" /> : <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center text-orange-600 font-semibold">{(p.name||"?").slice(0,2)}</div>}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{p.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{SERVICE_CATEGORIES.find(c=>c.id===p.category)?.name}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1">{p.area}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-3">{p.charges}</p>
            <Badge tone={p.status === "live" ? "green" : p.status === "pending" ? "amber" : p.status === "suspended" ? "red" : "default"}>
              {p.status === "live" ? "Live" : p.status === "pending" ? "Pending" : p.status === "suspended" ? "Suspended" : p.status}
            </Badge>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3 flex-wrap">
              {p.status === "pending" && <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setStatus(p.id, "live")}>Approve</PrimaryButton>}
              {p.status === "live" && <GhostButton small onClick={() => setStatus(p.id, "suspended")}>Suspend</GhostButton>}
              {p.status === "suspended" && <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setStatus(p.id, "live")}>Reactivate</PrimaryButton>}
              <GhostButton small onClick={() => openEdit(p)}>Edit</GhostButton>
              <button onClick={() => setProviders((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== p.id))} className="text-xs text-red-500 underline px-2">Deactivate</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {editingId && editForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <p className="font-display font-bold">Edit Provider</p>
              <button onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}><X size={18} className="text-stone-400" /></button>
            </div>
            <input value={editForm.name} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Name"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input value={editForm.area} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, area: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Areas covered"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <input value={editForm.charges} onChange={(e) => setEditForm((f) => ({ ...f, charges: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Charges"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={saveEdit}>Save</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminShopkeeperManagement({ orders }) {
  const [commission, setCommission] = useState(5);
  const [stores, setStores] = useState([
    { id: "st1", shop: "Rahim Store", category: "General Store", zone: "Gulberg", products: 12, status: "pending", balance: -600 },
    { id: "st2", shop: "Al-Madina Grocers", category: "Grocery", zone: "DHA", products: 34, status: "live", balance: 0 },
    { id: "st3", shop: "City Pharmacy", category: "Pharmacy", zone: "Bahria Town", products: 8, status: "pending", balance: 320 },
  ]);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(null);
  const setStatus = (id, status) => setStores((prev) => prev.map((s) => s.id === id ? { ...s, status } : s));
  const settle = (id) => setStores((prev) => prev.map((s) => s.id === id ? { ...s, balance: 0 } : s));

  const shopkeeperOrders = orders.filter((o) => o.type === "shopkeeper");
  const totalOwedByShops = stores.filter(s => s.balance < 0).reduce((s, x) => s + Math.abs(x.balance), 0);
  const totalOwedToShops = stores.filter(s => s.balance > 0).reduce((s, x) => s + x.balance, 0);

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Shopkeeper Management</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">All shopkeepers — stores, orders, wallets & settlements in one place</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Total Shopkeepers" value={String(stores.length)} icon={Store} />
        <StatCard label="Manual Orders" value={String(shopkeeperOrders.length)} icon={Package} />
        <StatCard label="Shopkeepers owe us" value={fmt(totalOwedByShops)} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
        <StatCard label="We owe shopkeepers" value={fmt(totalOwedToShops)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2">Platform Commission (Store/Menu orders)</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-3">Applied to every order placed through a shopkeeper's store catalog</p>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input value={commission} onChange={(e) => setCommission(e.target.value)} type="number"
            className="w-24 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          <span className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">%</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {stores.map((s) => (
          <div key={s.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <button onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === s.id ? null : s.id)} className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 text-left">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Store size={17} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.shop}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.category} · {s.zone} · {s.products} products</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Badge tone={s.status === "live" ? "green" : s.status === "pending" ? "amber" : "red"}>{s.status}</Badge>
                <ChevronRight size={15} className={`text-stone-300 transition ${expanded === s.id ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
              </div>
            </button>
            {expanded === s.id && (
              <div className="px-4 pb-4 space-y-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 pt-3">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                  <div className="bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg p-2.5"><p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Wallet balance</p><p className={`font-mono-tk font-semibold ${s.balance < 0 ? "text-red-500" : s.balance > 0 ? "text-emerald-600" : ""}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(s.balance))}</p></div>
                  <div className="bg-stone-50 dark:bg-stone-700 rounded-lg p-2.5"><p className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Manual orders</p><p className="font-mono-tk font-semibold">{shopkeeperOrders.filter(o => o.pickup?.shopName === s.shop).length}</p></div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  {s.status === "pending" && (
                    <>
                      <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setStatus(s.id, "live")}>Approve</PrimaryButton>
                      <GhostButton small onClick={() => setStatus(s.id, "rejected")}>Reject</GhostButton>
                    </>
                  )}
                  {s.status === "live" && <GhostButton small onClick={() => setStatus(s.id, "rejected")}>Take offline</GhostButton>}
                  {s.balance !== 0 && <GhostButton small onClick={() => settle(s.id)}>Settle wallet</GhostButton>}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminDisputes({ t }) {
  const [disputes, setDisputes] = useState([
    { id: "D-01", order: "TK-1039", reason: "Bill amount higher than expected", status: "open" },
    { id: "D-02", order: "TK-1021", reason: "Item not delivered as described", status: "resolved" },
  ]);
  const resolve = (id, outcome) => setDisputes((prev) => prev.map((d) => d.id === id ? { ...d, status: "resolved", outcome } : d));

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">{t.disputes}</h2>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {disputes.map((d) => (
          <div key={d.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{d.order} <span className="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-mono-tk text-xs ml-2">{d.id}</span></p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{d.reason}</p>
                {d.outcome && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 mt-1">Resolved: {d.outcome}</p>}
              </div>
              <Badge tone={d.status === "open" ? "amber" : "green"}>{d.status}</Badge>
            </div>
            {d.status === "open" && (
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700">
                <GhostButton small full onClick={() => resolve(d.id, "Refunded to customer wallet")}>Refund customer</GhostButton>
                <GhostButton small full onClick={() => resolve(d.id, "Charged to rider wallet")}>Charge rider</GhostButton>
                <PrimaryButton small full onClick={() => resolve(d.id, "Dismissed — bill confirmed correct")}>Dismiss</PrimaryButton>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="pt-2">
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldAlert size={18} className="text-red-500" /> Fraud Detection Alerts</h3>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[
            { customer: "Ayesha Khan", flag: "5 order cancellations in the last 7 days", severity: "high" },
            { customer: "Hassan Raza", flag: "3 disputed bills in a row, always claiming overcharge", severity: "medium" },
          ].map((f, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-red-100 dark:border-red-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{f.customer}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">{f.flag}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Badge tone={f.severity === "high" ? "red" : "amber"}>{f.severity}</Badge>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminWallets({ t, riders, setRiders, withdrawals, setWithdrawals, adminAccount }) {
  const [section, setSection] = useState("company"); // company | riders | customers | shopkeepers | withdrawals | approvals
  const [toast, setToast] = useState("");
  const showToast = (msg) => { setToast(msg); setTimeout(() => setToast(""), 3000); };

  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState([
    { id: "c1", name: "Sana Ahmed", phone: "0300 1234567", balance: -150 },
    { id: "c2", name: "Hassan Raza", phone: "0321 5567890", balance: 320 },
    { id: "c3", name: "Ayesha Khan", phone: "0333 9988776", balance: 0 },
  ]);
  const [shopkeepers, setShopkeepers] = useState([
    { id: "s1", name: "Rahim Store", phone: "0333 2211445", balance: -600 },
    { id: "s2", name: "Al-Madina Grocers", phone: "0345 1122334", balance: 0 },
  ]);
  const [pendingApprovals, setPendingApprovals] = useState([
    { id: "p1", name: "Hassan Raza", type: "Customer", amount: 500, note: "Top-up screenshot submitted" },
  ]);
  const [requestTarget, setRequestTarget] = useState(null); // {name, type} for showing account details

  const settleCustomer = (id) => setCustomers((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.id === id ? { ...c, balance: 0 } : c));
  const settleShopkeeper = (id) => setShopkeepers((prev) => prev.map((s) => s.id === id ? { ...s, balance: 0 } : s));
  const settleRider = (id) => setRiders((prev) => prev.map((r) => r.id === id ? { ...r, wallet: 0 } : r));
  const [payoutModal, setPayoutModal] = useState(null); // withdrawal id being paid out
  const approveWithdrawal = (id, screenshotUrl) => setWithdrawals((prev) => prev.map((w) => w.id === id ? { ...w, status: "awaiting_confirmation", screenshotUrl } : w));
  const approvePending = (id, list, setList) => {
    const entry = list.find((p) => p.id === id);
    if (entry.type === "Customer") setCustomers((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.name === entry.name ? { ...c, balance: c.balance + entry.amount } : c));
    setPendingApprovals((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
  };

  const companyRevenue = 64200 + 51300 + 47800; // demo aggregate
  const totalOwedToRiders = riders.reduce((s, r) => s + Math.max(r.wallet, 0), 0);
  const totalOwedByCustomers = customers.filter(c => c.balance < 0).reduce((s, c) => s + Math.abs(c.balance), 0);
  const totalOwedToCustomers = customers.filter(c => c.balance > 0).reduce((s, c) => s + c.balance, 0);

  const sections = [
    { key: "company", label: "Company" },
    { key: "riders", label: "Riders" },
    { key: "customers", label: "Customers" },
    { key: "shopkeepers", label: "Shopkeepers" },
    { key: "withdrawals", label: "Withdraw Requests" },
    { key: "approvals", label: `Pending Approvals${pendingApprovals.length ? ` (${pendingApprovals.length})` : ""}` },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Wallets</h2>

      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {sections.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.key} onClick={() => setSection(s.key)}
            className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${section === s.key ? "border-orange-400 bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-700" : "border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300"}`}>
            {s.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {section === "company" && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
            <StatCard label="Company net revenue" value={fmt(companyRevenue)} icon={Wallet} />
            <StatCard label="Owed to riders" value={fmt(totalOwedToRiders)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
            <StatCard label="Owed by customers" value={fmt(totalOwedByCustomers)} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
            <StatCard label="Owed to customers" value={fmt(totalOwedToCustomers)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 leading-relaxed">
              Taskly acts as the settlement hub — riders and customers never settle directly with each other.
              If a customer underpays, the company covers the rider's shortfall and the customer's wallet goes negative (settled on their next order).
              If a customer overpays, the company refunds the customer and recovers the excess from the rider's wallet.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {section === "riders" && (() => {
        const owesUs = riders.filter((r) => r.wallet < 0);
        const weOwe = riders.filter((r) => r.wallet > 0);
        return (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <StatCard label="Riders owe us" value={fmt(owesUs.reduce((s,r)=>s+Math.abs(r.wallet),0))} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
              <StatCard label="We owe riders" value={fmt(totalOwedToRiders)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-600 mb-2">Riders who owe Taskly</p>
              <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-stone-700">
                {owesUs.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 p-4">Nobody owes Taskly right now</p>}
                {owesUs.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{r.name}</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="font-mono-tk text-red-500 text-sm">{fmt(Math.abs(r.wallet))}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => setRequestTarget({ name: r.name, type: "rider" })} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Request Payment</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-600 mb-2">Riders Taskly owes</p>
              <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-stone-700">
                {weOwe.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 p-4">Nothing owed to riders right now</p>}
                {weOwe.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{r.name}</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="font-mono-tk text-emerald-600 text-sm">{fmt(r.wallet)}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => settleRider(r.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Payout</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })()}

      {section === "customers" && (() => {
        const owesUs = customers.filter((c) => c.balance < 0);
        const weOwe = customers.filter((c) => c.balance > 0);
        return (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <StatCard label="Customers owe us" value={fmt(totalOwedByCustomers)} icon={ArrowDownRight} />
              <StatCard label="We owe customers" value={fmt(totalOwedToCustomers)} icon={ArrowUpRight} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-600 mb-2">Customers who owe Taskly</p>
              <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-stone-700">
                {owesUs.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 p-4">Nobody owes Taskly right now</p>}
                {owesUs.map((c) => (
                  <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                    <div><p className="text-sm font-medium">{c.name}</p><p className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono-tk">{c.phone}</p></div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="font-mono-tk text-red-500 text-sm">{fmt(Math.abs(c.balance))}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => setRequestTarget({ name: c.name, type: "customer" })} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Request Payment</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-600 mb-2">Customers Taskly owes (refund due)</p>
              <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-stone-700">
                {weOwe.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 p-4">No refunds pending</p>}
                {weOwe.map((c) => (
                  <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                    <div><p className="text-sm font-medium">{c.name}</p><p className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono-tk">{c.phone}</p></div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="font-mono-tk text-emerald-600 text-sm">{fmt(c.balance)}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => settleCustomer(c.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Payout</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })()}

      {section === "shopkeepers" && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 text-xs">
                <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Shopkeeper</th>
                <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Phone</th>
                <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Wallet balance</th>
                <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Status</th>
                <th className="text-left font-medium px-5 py-3">Action</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {shopkeepers.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.id} className="border-b border-stone-50">
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">{s.name}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{s.phone}</td>
                  <td className={`px-5 py-3 font-mono-tk ${s.balance < 0 ? "text-red-500" : ""}`}>{fmt(Math.abs(s.balance))}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">
                    <Badge tone={s.balance < 0 ? "red" : s.balance > 0 ? "amber" : "green"}>
                      {s.balance < 0 ? "Shopkeeper owes" : s.balance > 0 ? "Payout due" : "Settled"}
                    </Badge>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">
                    {s.balance !== 0 && <button onClick={() => settleShopkeeper(s.id)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Mark settled</button>}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {section === "withdrawals" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {withdrawals.map((w) => (
            <div key={w.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{w.rider}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 font-mono-tk">{fmt(w.amount)}</p>
              </div>
              {w.status === "pending" && <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setPayoutModal(w.id)}>Approve & Pay</PrimaryButton>}
              {w.status === "awaiting_confirmation" && <Badge tone="amber">Awaiting rider confirmation</Badge>}
              {w.status === "paid" && <Badge tone="green">Confirmed ✓</Badge>}
              {w.status === "rejected" && <Badge tone="red">Rider rejected</Badge>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {payoutModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full space-y-3">
            <p className="font-semibold">Confirm payout</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">Transfer the amount from Taskly's account to the rider's account, then upload proof — the rider will confirm receipt on their end.</p>
            <label className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl py-8 cursor-pointer text-stone-400 hover:border-orange-300 hover:text-orange-500">
              <Camera size={22} /><span className="text-xs">Upload transfer screenshot</span>
              <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => {
                const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                if (f) { approveWithdrawal(payoutModal, URL.createObjectURL(f)); setPayoutModal(null); }
              }} />
            </label>
            <GhostButton full onClick={() => setPayoutModal(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {section === "approvals" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Screenshots submitted by customers/riders after transferring to the company account — review & approve</p>
          {pendingApprovals.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 p-4">No pending approvals</p>}
          {pendingApprovals.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id} className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-stone-100 dark:bg-stone-700 flex items-center justify-center"><Camera size={16} className="text-stone-400" /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{p.name} <Badge tone="default">{p.type}</Badge></p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">{p.note} · {fmt(p.amount)}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => approvePending(p.id, pendingApprovals, setPendingApprovals)}>Approve</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {requestTarget && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 z-40 flex items-center justify-center px-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full space-y-3 text-center">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-orange-50 dark:bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto"><Send size={18} className="text-orange-500" /></div>
            <p className="font-semibold">Send payment request to {requestTarget.name}?</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400">They'll receive Taskly's account details ({adminAccount.bankName} — {adminAccount.accountNumber}) with instructions to transfer and upload a screenshot for approval.</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
              <GhostButton full onClick={() => setRequestTarget(null)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
              <PrimaryButton full onClick={() => { showToast(`Request + account details sent to ${requestTarget.name}`); setRequestTarget(null); }}>Send Request</PrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminAccountCard({ account, setAccount }) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(account);

  const save = () => { setAccount(form); setEditing(false); };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Company Account Details</p>
        {!editing && <button onClick={() => { setForm(account); setEditing(true); }} className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">Edit</button>}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-3">Shown to customers, riders & shopkeepers whenever a payment needs to go directly to Taskly</p>

      {!editing ? (
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <CopyField label="Bank" value={account.bankName} copyable={false} />
          <CopyField label="Account Holder" value={account.accountHolder} copyable={false} />
          <CopyField label="Account Number" value={account.accountNumber} />
          <CopyField label="IBAN" value={account.iban} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Bank name</label>
            <input value={form.bankName} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, bankName: e.target.value }))}
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Account holder name</label>
            <input value={form.accountHolder} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, accountHolder: e.target.value }))}
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">Account number</label>
            <input value={form.accountNumber} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, accountNumber: e.target.value }))}
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 mb-1 block">IBAN</label>
            <input value={form.iban} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, iban: e.target.value }))}
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
            <GhostButton small full onClick={() => setEditing(false)}>Cancel</GhostButton>
            <PrimaryButton small full onClick={save}>Save</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminSettings({ t, adminAccount, setAdminAccount }) {
  const [waEnabled, setWaEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [smsEnabled, setSmsEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState("");
  const [apiKeySaved, setApiKeySaved] = useState(false);
  const [subAdmins, setSubAdmins] = useState([
    { name: "Fahad Malik", role: "Orders Manager" },
    { name: "Ayesha Noor", role: "Zone Manager — DHA" },
  ]);
  const [showStaffForm, setShowStaffForm] = useState(false);
  const [staffName, setStaffName] = useState(""); const [staffRole, setStaffRole] = useState("Orders Manager");
  const [legalOpen, setLegalOpen] = useState(null);

  const addStaff = () => {
    if (!staffName) return;
    setSubAdmins((prev) => [...prev, { name: staffName, role: staffRole }]);
    setStaffName(""); setShowStaffForm(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Settings</h2>

      <AdminAccountCard account={adminAccount} setAccount={setAdminAccount} />

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-3">Messaging Gateway</p>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div><p className="text-sm">WhatsApp Business API</p><p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Auto marketing message to new customer numbers</p></div>
            <button onClick={() => setWaEnabled(v => !v)} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full transition relative ${waEnabled ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-200"}`}>
              <span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-5 h-5 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-full transition ${waEnabled ? "left-5" : "left-0.5"}`} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div><p className="text-sm">SMS Gateway</p><p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Fallback when WhatsApp isn't available</p></div>
            <button onClick={() => setSmsEnabled(v => !v)} className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full transition relative ${smsEnabled ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-stone-200"}`}>
              <span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-5 h-5 bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 rounded-full transition ${smsEnabled ? "left-5" : "left-0.5"}`} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mt-1">
            <input value={apiKey} onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)} placeholder="API Key"
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
            <button onClick={() => { setApiKeySaved(true); setTimeout(() => setApiKeySaved(false), 2000); }}
              className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 shrink-0">{apiKeySaved ? "Saved!" : "Save"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-1">Announcements moved</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">Zone & user-type targeted announcements now live inside <span className="font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">Customer Management → Announcements</span> and <span className="font-medium text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300">Rider Management → Announcements</span>, so a message only reaches the zone and audience you actually pick.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Sub-Admins & Staff</p>
          <button onClick={() => setShowStaffForm((s) => !s)} className="text-xs text-orange-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Plus size={13} /> Add staff</button>
        </div>
        {showStaffForm && (
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
            <input value={staffName} onChange={(e) => setStaffName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Name"
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <select value={staffRole} onChange={(e) => setStaffRole(e.target.value)} className="border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 outline-none">
              <option>Orders Manager</option>
              <option>Zone Manager — Gulberg</option>
              <option>Zone Manager — DHA</option>
              <option>Support Staff</option>
            </select>
            <PrimaryButton small onClick={addStaff}>Add</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {subAdmins.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between border border-stone-100 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3">
              <p className="text-sm">{s.name}</p>
              <Badge tone="orange">{s.role}</Badge>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2">Legal</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
          <GhostButton small onClick={() => setLegalOpen(legalOpen === "terms" ? null : "terms")}>Edit Terms & Conditions</GhostButton>
          <GhostButton small onClick={() => setLegalOpen(legalOpen === "privacy" ? null : "privacy")}>Edit Privacy Policy</GhostButton>
        </div>
        {legalOpen && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <textarea rows={4} defaultValue={legalOpen === "terms"
              ? "By using Taskly, riders purchase items on your behalf at prevailing market prices..."
              : "Taskly collects phone numbers, addresses, and order details to provide delivery service..."}
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
            <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setLegalOpen(null)}>Save changes</PrimaryButton>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminPromos({ t }) {
  const [promos, setPromos] = useState([
    { code: "TASKLY100", off: "Rs. 100 off", expiry: "31 Aug 2026" },
    { code: "FIRST20", off: "20% off first order", expiry: "No expiry" },
  ]);
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [code, setCode] = useState(""); const [off, setOff] = useState(""); const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState("");

  const create = () => {
    if (!code || !off) return;
    setPromos((p) => [...p, { code: code.toUpperCase(), off, expiry: expiry || "No expiry" }]);
    setCode(""); setOff(""); setExpiry(""); setShowForm(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 md:p-8 space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">{t.promoCodes}</h2>
        <PrimaryButton small onClick={() => setShowForm((s) => !s)}><Plus size={14} /> Create Code</PrimaryButton>
      </div>
      {showForm && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-2xl p-5 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 items-end">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1 block">Code</label>
            <input value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)} placeholder="SUMMER25"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400 font-mono-tk" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1 block">Discount</label>
            <input value={off} onChange={(e) => setOff(e.target.value)} placeholder="Rs. 50 off / 10% off"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-stone-500 dark:text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mb-1 block">Expiry</label>
            <input value={expiry} onChange={(e) => setExpiry(e.target.value)} placeholder="31 Dec 2026"
              className="w-full border border-stone-200 dark:border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-orange-400" />
          </div>
          <PrimaryButton small onClick={create}>Create</PrimaryButton>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {promos.map((p) => (
          <TicketCard key={p.code} className="p-4">
            <p className="font-mono-tk font-bold text-orange-600">{p.code}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 dark:text-stone-300 mt-1">{p.off}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500 mt-2">Expires: {p.expiry}</p>
          </TicketCard>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
